📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期猴痘病毒(Mpox病毒)研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 在单靶标条件下,检测结果一致地检测到分支 I 和分支 II MPXV,浓度分别低至 6.8 × 10 -1 和 7.7 × 10 -1 拷贝/μL。 [10.1016/j.microc.2026.118902]。
  • 该程序从 597 名 猴痘 患者采集了 61,926 张标准化临床质量照片。 [10.1136/bmjgh-2025-020904]。
  • NTV-ΔF1L-C7L 在 疫苗 生产细胞系中表现出显着增强的复制,与亲本 NTV 相比,MRC-5 细胞中的病毒产量增加了 680 倍以上;其对小鼠的致病力显着降低,与痘苗病毒天坛株(VTT)的致病力相比下降10倍以上;两次肌肉注射后,NTV-ΔF1L-C7L 引发高滴度的 正痘病毒 特异性 IgG 和针对牛痘和猴痘病毒的中和抗体,以及表现出 Th1 偏好的强大细胞 免疫应答,其明显强于亲本 NTV 或 VTT 疫苗接种 诱导的效果。
  • 在 419 名参与者中,46.8%(95% CI:42.0–51.7)具有良好的知识,而 43.4%(95% CI:39.1–48.0)对 猴痘 表现出积极态度。 [10.1002/hsr2.72854]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • Under single-target conditions, the assays consistently detected clade I and clade II MPXV at concentrations as low as 6.8 × 10 −1 and 7.7 × 10 −1 copies/μL, respectively. [10.1016/j.microc.2026.118902].
  • The procedures enabled the acquisition of 61,926 standardised, clinical-quality photographs from 597 patients with mpox. [10.1136/bmjgh-2025-020904].
  • NTV-ΔF1L-C7L exhibited significantly enhanced replication in vaccine production cell lines, with viral yields increasing by more than 680-fold in MRC-5 cells compared to those of the parental NTV; its pathogenicity in mice was significantly reduced, showing more than a 10-fold decrease compared to the pathogenicity of the vaccinia virus Tiantan strain (VTT); following two intramuscular doses, NTV-ΔF1L-C7L elicited high titers of orthopoxvirus-specific IgG and neutralizing antibodies against vaccinia and monkeypox viruses, along with a robust cellular immune response exhibiting a Th1 bias, which was significantly stronger than that induced by either parental NTV or VTT vaccination.
  • Of the 419 participants, 46.8% (95% CI: 42.0–51.7) had good knowledge, while 43.4% (95% CI: 39.1–48.0) showed positive attitudes toward mpox. [10.1002/hsr2.72854].

📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • 本期猴痘病毒(Mpox病毒)新闻集中呈现以下动态。
  • 确诊为 I 支猴痘病毒 (MPXV) 暴发,包括 Ia 和 Ib 支支,有实验室确诊病例,多个地区持续存在本地 传播。该事件由疾病预防控制中心正式报道。 [news-c601928238adb823]。
  • 几内亚卫生部在 CDC 支持下报告了确诊的猴痘 暴发(分支 II)。事件状态:已确认 暴发 并正在进行响应。 [news-6a6cef4545ec1c8a]。
  • 实验室确诊的 猴痘 病例最初表现为严重急性扁桃体炎,伴有 CT 确诊的口咽部狭窄,随后出现特征性皮肤病变。该案例是按照 CARE 指南进行的案例研究报告的。 [news-ba13f07d88bbf309]。
  • 媒介 中心正在同时开发三种 mRNA 疫苗——对抗 HIV、猴痘和蜱传脑炎。这是一项研究进展,而不是 暴发 或已确认的事件。 [news-72d9a8796bbf0425]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following Mpox virus news developments.
  • Confirmed outbreaks of clade I monkeypox virus (MPXV), including subclades Ia and Ib, with laboratory-confirmed cases and sustained local transmission in multiple regions. The event is officially reported by the CDC.
  • Confirmed monkeypox outbreak (clade II) reported by Guinea's Ministry of Health with CDC support. Event status: Confirmed outbreak with ongoing response. [news-6a6cef4545ec1c8a].
  • Laboratory-confirmed mpox case presenting initially as severe acute tonsillitis with CT-confirmed oropharyngeal narrowing, before characteristic cutaneous lesions developed.
  • The Vector centre is developing three mRNA vaccines simultaneously – against HIV, monkeypox and tick-borne encephalitis. This is a research development, not an outbreak or confirmed event. [news-72d9a8796bbf0425].

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: BMJ global health  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-07-30  |  DOI: 10.1136/bmjgh-2025-020904  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 11, 7, e020904
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

全身摄影,用于刚果民主共和国偏远研究地点 猴痘 皮肤损伤的标准化记录。

Total body photography for standardised documentation of mpox skin lesions in remote study sites in the Democratic Republic of the Congo.
作者： Inga Saknite, Patrick Mutombo Lupola, Andrew J McNeil, Sylva Kasereka Sivasingana, Jean Marc Tshilombo Mwindila, Elie Malamba Lohalo, Olga Divine Tshi-Manina, Fiston Lodi Ndjovu, Celestin Tshimanga, Baudouin Matiba Ngalumono
摘要中文翻译我们为 PALM007(Pamoja Tulinde Maisha - 斯瓦希里语共同拯救生命)随机临床试验设计并成功实施了摄影协议,该试验评估了刚果民主共和国 (DRC) 的 特考韦瑞 和 猴痘 的 治疗。我们解决了在远程研究 治疗 中心站点的高风险“红区”对 猴痘 患者进行标准化摄影程序的独特挑战和局限性。主要考虑因素包括标准化摄影协议、培训、质量保证、参与者隐私和舒适度以及人际沟通。我们开发的程序能够采集 597 名 猴痘 患者的 61 926 张标准化临床质量照片。这些考虑因素可能会指导未来在资源匮乏的环境中收集标准化照片的研究。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这项研究需要解决在刚果民主共和国偏远高风险环境中为 猴痘 患者进行标准化摄影程序的挑战。
设计与对象
该研究是 PALM007 随机临床试验的一部分,该试验在刚果民主共和国的远程研究 治疗 中心评估 特考韦瑞 对于 猴痘 治疗 的效果。
核心方法
主要方法包括制定标准化摄影协议、培训、质量保证措施、确保参与者的隐私和舒适度以及在充满挑战的环境中管理人际沟通。
主要结果
该程序从 597 名 猴痘 患者采集了 61,926 张标准化临床质量照片。
解释与创新
作者将开发的程序解释为克服远程环境中独特挑战的成功方法,并指出它们在资源匮乏的环境中实现高质量文档的新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为未来在资源匮乏地区收集标准化照片的研究提供信息,改进对 猴痘 等传染病的记录和监测。
局限与证据强度
由于这是一项随机对照试验,证据强度很高,尽管其局限性包括在偏远、高风险的“红色区域”进行手术的挑战。

Total body photography for standardised documentation of mpox skin lesions in remote study sites in the Democratic Republic of the Congo.

Authors: Inga Saknite, Patrick Mutombo Lupola, Andrew J McNeil, Sylva Kasereka Sivasingana, Jean Marc Tshilombo Mwindila, Elie Malamba Lohalo, Olga Divine Tshi-Manina, Fiston Lodi Ndjovu, Celestin Tshimanga, Baudouin Matiba Ngalumono
Original abstract (English)
We designed and successfully implemented photography protocols for the PALM007 (Pamoja Tulinde Maisha - Together Save Lives in Swahili) randomised clinical trial evaluating tecovirimat for the treatment of mpox in the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC). We addressed the unique challenges and limitations of conducting standardised photography procedures on patients with mpox in high-risk 'red zones' in remote study treatment centre sites. Key considerations included standardised photography protocols, training, quality assurance, participant privacy and comfort, and interpersonal communication. Our developed procedures enabled acquisition of 61 926 standardised, clinical-quality photographs of 597 patients with mpox. These considerations may guide future studies collecting standardised photographs in a low-resource setting.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was needed to address challenges in conducting standardised photography procedures for mpox patients in remote, high-risk settings in the Democratic Republic of the Congo.
Design and population
The study was conducted as part of the PALM007 randomised clinical trial evaluating tecovirimat for mpox treatment in remote study treatment centre sites in the DRC.
Core methods
Key methods included developing standardised photography protocols, training, quality assurance measures, ensuring participant privacy and comfort, and managing interpersonal communication in challenging environments.
Main results
The procedures enabled the acquisition of 61,926 standardised, clinical-quality photographs from 597 patients with mpox.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the developed procedures as a successful approach to overcome unique challenges in remote settings, noting their novelty in enabling high-quality documentation in low-resource environments.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings can inform future studies collecting standardised photographs in low-resource settings, improving documentation and monitoring of infectious diseases like mpox.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is high as this is a randomised controlled trial, though limitations include the challenges of conducting procedures in remote, high-risk 'red zones'.

不完美观察和重叠 传播 链下估计亚临界繁殖数的偏差:理论框架及其在 猴痘 中的应用

Bias in Estimating Subcritical Reproduction Numbers Under Imperfect Observation and Overlapping Transmission Chains: Theoretical Framework and Application to Mpox
作者： Seth Blumberg, Santiago D. Cárdenas, Andrew W Liu, Taye Samuel Faniran, Juliet R.C. Pulliam, James O. Lloyd-Smith, James O. Lloyd‐Smith
摘要中文翻译摘要 估计新发感染的 传播 潜在和危险因素通常依赖于不完整的 监测 数据。当感染被遗漏或其来源被错误分类时,对有效繁殖数(Rs)以及病例属性与感染源之间关联的估计可能会有偏差。我们开发了一个分析框架来量化和纠正这些偏差,将 传播 链的不完美观察和纠缠视为错误分类的形式,从而扭曲 传播 参数的估计。偏差取决于病例观察的概率 (Pobs) 和同时引入的程度 (Rp)。我们使用刚果民主共和国 猴痘 的 猴痘 数据(1981-1986、2013-2017)来说明该框架,表明不完整的观察会导致卢比的低估,而重叠的引入可能会导致高估。这两种类型的偏差也会减弱区分原发性(跨物种溢出)和继发性(人与人)感染的特征的比值比。考虑到这些偏差,为 2022 年 猴痘 在全球出现以及当前 传播 模式与历史趋势有何不同提供了背景。这些结果表明,错误分类和缺失的标准流行病学概念如何能够澄清 传播 研究中的偏差,并改善对新出现病原体(包括人畜共患病和 疫苗 可预防感染)的 监测 数据的解释。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
估计新发感染的 传播 潜在和风险因素通常依赖于不完整的 监测 数据,这可能导致对有效传染数 (Rs) 以及病例属性和感染源之间关联的估计出现偏差。
设计与对象
该研究开发了一个分析框架来量化和纠正由于 传播 链的不完美观察和纠缠而引起的偏差,将这些视为错误分类的形式,扭曲了 传播 参数的估计。
核心方法
该框架根据病例观察概率 (Pobs) 和同时引入的程度 (Rp) 量化偏差,并应用于刚果民主共和国的 猴痘 监测 数据(1981-1986 年、2013-2017 年)。
主要结果
不完整的观察会导致Rs的低估,而重叠的介绍会导致Rs的高估;这两种类型的偏差也会减弱区分原发感染(跨物种溢出)和继发(人与人)感染的特征的比值比。
解释与创新
作者展示了错误分类和缺失的标准流行病学概念如何澄清 传播 研究中的偏差,为 2022 年 猴痘 在全球出现以及与历史趋势的差异提供背景。
科研与公卫意义
考虑到这些偏差可以改善对新兴病原体(包括人畜共患病和 疫苗 可预防感染)的 监测 数据的解释,并为风险评估和控制策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究依赖于理论框架和历史监测数据,在完整性和准确性方面可能存在固有的局限性。

Bias in Estimating Subcritical Reproduction Numbers Under Imperfect Observation and Overlapping Transmission Chains: Theoretical Framework and Application to Mpox

Authors: Seth Blumberg, Santiago D. Cárdenas, Andrew W Liu, Taye Samuel Faniran, Juliet R.C. Pulliam, James O. Lloyd-Smith, James O. Lloyd‐Smith
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Estimating transmission potential and risk factors for emerging infections often relies on incomplete surveillance data. When infections are missed or their sources are misclassified, estimates of the effective reproduction number (Rs) and associations between case attributes and infection source may be biased. We developed an analytic framework to quantify and correct these biases, treating imperfect observation and entanglement of transmission chains as forms of misclassification that distort estimates of transmission parameters. The bias depends on the probability of case observation (Pobs) and the extent of concurrent introductions (Rp). We illustrate this framework using surveillance data for mpox in the Democratic Republic of the Congo (1981–1986, 2013–2017), showing that incomplete observation leads to underestimation of Rs, whereas overlapping introductions can cause overestimation. Both types of bias also attenuate odds ratios for traits that distinguish primary (spillover) from secondary (human-tohuman) infections. Accounting for these biases provides context for the global emergence of mpox in 2022 and for how current transmission patterns differ from historical trends. These results demonstrate how standard epidemiologic concepts of misclassification and missingness can clarify bias in transmission studies and improve interpretation of surveillance data for emerging pathogens, including zoonoses and vaccine-preventable infections.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Estimating transmission potential and risk factors for emerging infections often relies on incomplete surveillance data, which can lead to biased estimates of the effective reproduction number (Rs) and associations between case attributes and infection source.
Design and population
The study developed an analytic framework to quantify and correct biases caused by imperfect observation and entanglement of transmission chains, treating these as forms of misclassification that distort estimates of transmission parameters.
Core methods
The framework quantifies bias based on the probability of case observation (Pobs) and the extent of concurrent introductions (Rp), and was applied to mpox surveillance data from the Democratic Republic of the Congo (1981–1986, 2013–2017).
Main results
Incomplete observation leads to underestimation of Rs, whereas overlapping introductions can cause overestimation; both types of bias also attenuate odds ratios for traits distinguishing primary (spillover) from secondary (human-to-human) infections.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors demonstrate how standard epidemiologic concepts of misclassification and missingness can clarify bias in transmission studies, providing context for the global emergence of mpox in 2022 and differences from historical trends.
Scientific and public-health significance
Accounting for these biases improves interpretation of surveillance data for emerging pathogens, including zoonoses and vaccine-preventable infections, and informs risk assessment and control strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the study relies on theoretical framework and historical surveillance data, which may have inherent limitations in completeness and accuracy.

秘鲁利马 猴痘 和 猴痘-HIV 混合感染的临床特征:回顾性多中心分析

Clinical Characteristics of Mpox and Mpox– HIV Coinfection in Lima, Peru: A Retrospective Multicenter Analysis
作者： Neil Rupani, Alberto Mendoza, Jimena Ruiz, Frine Samalvides, Ana M. Quintana, Alberto Miranda, Augusto Tarazona, Laura R. Orellana, Carole Mitnick, Molly F. Franke
摘要中文翻译目的:本研究旨在评估 2022 年至 2023 年 暴发 期间秘鲁利马 猴痘 和 猴痘-HIV 双重感染的临床特征和结果。此外,它还试图确定住院的预测因素,特别是与艾滋病毒免疫抑制相关的预测因素。方法:我们对 2022 年 6 月至 2023 年 1 月期间在秘鲁利马三所公立转诊医院确诊的 588 名 猴痘 患者进行了回顾性分析。数据从 猴痘 猴痘 表格、医疗图表和实验室系统中提取。进行描述性统计和双变量分析,然后进行多变量逻辑回归以确定住院的预测因素。结果:588 名 猴痘 患者中,平均年龄为 32.1 岁,其中 54.8% 为 HIV 感染者(PLWH)。8% 的人住院,其中 PLWH 的住院率 (11.2%) 高于未感染 HIV 的人 (4.1%)。直肠炎和 CD4 计数 < 500 个细胞/μL 是住院的显着预测因素(调整后的比值比 [aOR]:分别为 7.47 和 5.74)。感染者和感染者的病变计数较低,并且观察到明显的皮疹分布。发生六例死亡,全部为感染者,其中败血症是主要原因。结论:这项研究强调了利马感染者和未感染 HIV 的人之间 猴痘 的临床差异,即使是轻微的 HIV 相关免疫抑制(CD4 < 500 个细胞/μL)和直肠炎也是住院的关键因素。死亡仅发生在感染者中,这强调了有效管理艾滋病毒以减轻严重 猴痘 后果的重要性。加强艾滋病毒护理并确保获得 猴痘 治疗 对于未来 暴发 的应对策略至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估 2022 年至 2023 年 暴发 期间秘鲁利马 猴痘 和 猴痘-HIV 双重感染的临床特征和结果。此外,它还试图确定住院的预测因素,特别是与艾滋病毒免疫抑制相关的预测因素。
设计与对象
我们对 2022 年 6 月至 2023 年 1 月期间秘鲁利马三家公立转诊医院的 588 名确诊为 猴痘 的患者进行了回顾性分析。
核心方法
进行描述性统计和双变量分析,然后进行多变量逻辑回归以确定住院的预测因素。数据是从 猴痘 监测 表格、医疗图表和实验室系统中提取的。
主要结果
588 名 猴痘 患者中,平均年龄为 32.1 岁,其中 54.8% 为艾滋病病毒感染者(PLWH)。8% 的人住院,其中 PLWH 的住院率 (11.2%) 高于未感染 HIV 的人 (4.1%)。直肠炎和 CD4 计数 < 500 个细胞/μL 是住院的显着预测因素(调整后的比值比 [aOR]:分别为 7.47 和 5.74)。感染者和感染者的病变计数较低,并且观察到明显的皮疹分布。发生六例死亡,全部为感染者,其中败血症是主要原因。
解释与创新
这项研究强调了利马感染者和未感染 HIV 的人之间 猴痘 的临床差异,即使是轻微的 HIV 相关免疫抑制(CD4 < 500 个细胞/μL)和直肠炎也是住院的关键因素。死亡仅发生在感染者中,这强调了有效管理艾滋病毒以减轻严重 猴痘 后果的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
加强艾滋病毒护理并确保获得 猴痘 治疗 对于未来 暴发 的应对策略至关重要。这些发现可以为 HIV 感染者的针对性 监测 和 猴痘 干预工作提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究是回顾性的,基于 监测 表格、医疗图表和实验室系统,这可能会引入偏差或缺失数据。由于研究的观察性质和潜在的混杂因素,证据的强度适中。

Clinical Characteristics of Mpox and Mpox– HIV Coinfection in Lima, Peru: A Retrospective Multicenter Analysis

Authors: Neil Rupani, Alberto Mendoza, Jimena Ruiz, Frine Samalvides, Ana M. Quintana, Alberto Miranda, Augusto Tarazona, Laura R. Orellana, Carole Mitnick, Molly F. Franke
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVES: This study aimed to assess the clinical characteristics and outcomes of mpox and mpox-HIV coinfection in Lima, Peru, during the 2022-2023 outbreak. Also, it sought to identify predictors of hospitalisation, particularly related to HIV immunosuppression. METHODS: We conducted a retrospective analysis of 588 individuals with confirmed mpox across three public referral hospitals in Lima, Peru, between June 2022 and January 2023. Data were extracted from mpox surveillance forms, medical charts, and laboratory systems. Descriptive statistics and bivariate analyses were performed, followed by multivariate logistic regression to identify predictors of hospitalisation. RESULTS: Among the 588 individuals with mpox, the mean age was 32.1 years, and 54.8% were people living with HIV (PLWH). Hospitalisation occurred in 8% of individuals, with a higher rate among PLWH (11.2%) compared to individuals without HIV (4.1%). Proctitis and a CD4 count < 500 cells/μL were significant predictors of hospitalisation (adjusted odds ratio [aOR]: 7.47 and 5.74, respectively). Lesion counts were lower in PLWH, and a distinct rash distribution was observed. Six deaths occurred, all in PLWH, with sepsis being the leading cause. CONCLUSIONS: This study highlights the clinical differences of mpox between PLWH and people without HIV in Lima, with even mild HIV-related immunosuppression (CD4 < 500 cells/μL) and proctitis being key factors for hospitalisation. Deaths occurred exclusively in PLWH, emphasising the importance of managing HIV effectively to mitigate severe mpox outcomes. Strengthening HIV care and ensuring access to mpox treatment are critical in response strategies for future outbreaks.

Question and background

猴痘 特考韦瑞 治疗 期间的临床病程和病毒学动态:日本前瞻性多中心病例系列

Clinical course and virologic dynamics of Mpox during tecovirimat treatment: Prospective multicenter case series in Japan
作者： Ayana Sakurai, Yutaro Akiyama, Shinichiro Morioka, Shinya Tsuzuki, Takato Nakamoto, Eri Inoue, Takahiro Aoki, Tomoki Yoshikawa, Masaya Yamato, Hideta Nakamura
摘要中文翻译简介:在本研究进行时,日本还没有批准用于 猴痘 的 治疗,并且 特考韦瑞 治疗 期间的临床病程和病毒学变化的数据有限。方法:我们对2022年6月28日至2025年3月31日期间PCR确诊的猴痘患者进行了前瞻性多中心观察病例系列,以描述特考韦瑞 14天疗程的临床病程和安全性。参与者可以选择治疗或不选择治疗。结果:31名参与者全部​​获得特考韦瑞;24 人完成了一个为期 14 天的疗程,2 人接受了多个疗程加痘苗免疫球蛋白。平均年龄为 39 岁(范围为 21-74 岁),全部为男性。19 名 (61.3%) 是 HIV 感染者,CD4 计数中位数为 531 个细胞/μL(范围为 50-830),其中 3 名 CD4 计数 <200 个细胞/μL。23 名参与者 (74.2%) 发生严重 猴痘,其中 3 名参与者 (9.7%) 具有严重疾病的危险因素,均因 CD4 < 200 个细胞/μL 所致。30 名参与者出现皮肤损伤。第 14 天,23 名参与者获得了皮肤 PCR 结果;16 人(69.6%)呈阴性。尽管一名晚期 HIV 感染患者在长期住院后死亡,但 14 或 30 天内没有发生死亡。2 名参与者发生不良事件,被认为与 治疗 无关。讨论:这个前瞻性多中心病例系列描述了 猴痘 的临床病程以及在现实世界实践中接受 特考韦瑞 的患者皮肤 PCR 阴性的时间动态。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
截至本研究进行时,日本尚无批准用于 猴痘 的 治疗,并且 特考韦瑞 治疗 期间的临床病程和病毒学变化的数据有限。
设计与对象
我们在2022年6月28日至2025年3月31日期间对PCR确诊的猴痘患者进行了前瞻性多中心观察病例系列,以描述特考韦瑞 14天疗程的临床病程和安全性。参与者可以选择治疗或不选择治疗。
核心方法
第 14 天,23 名参与者获得了皮肤 PCR 结果;16 人(69.6%)呈阴性。
主要结果
所有31名参与者均获得特考韦瑞;24 人完成了一个为期 14 天的疗程,2 人接受了多个疗程加痘苗免疫球蛋白。平均年龄为 39 岁(范围为 21-74 岁),全部为男性。19 名 (61.3%) 是 HIV 感染者,CD4 计数中位数为 531 个细胞/μL(范围为 50-830),其中 3 名 CD4 计数 <200 个细胞/μL。23 名参与者 (74.2%) 发生严重 猴痘,其中 3 名参与者 (9.7%) 具有严重疾病的危险因素,均因 CD4 < 200 个细胞/μL 所致。30 名参与者出现皮肤损伤。尽管一名晚期 HIV 感染患者在长期住院后死亡,但 14 或 30 天内没有发生死亡。2 名参与者发生不良事件,被认为与 治疗 无关。
解释与创新
这个前瞻性多中心病例系列描述了 猴痘 的临床病程以及在现实世界实践中接受 特考韦瑞 的患者皮肤 PCR 阴性的时间动态。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现提供了 特考韦瑞 治疗 期间 猴痘 的临床病程和病毒学动力学的真实世界证据,这可以为类似环境下的 治疗 指南和 监测 策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究是一项前瞻性多中心观察病例系列,样本量小,随访期有限。由于观察设计和潜在的选择偏差,证据强度中等。

Clinical course and virologic dynamics of Mpox during tecovirimat treatment: Prospective multicenter case series in Japan

Authors: Ayana Sakurai, Yutaro Akiyama, Shinichiro Morioka, Shinya Tsuzuki, Takato Nakamoto, Eri Inoue, Takahiro Aoki, Tomoki Yoshikawa, Masaya Yamato, Hideta Nakamura
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: At the time of this study, no approved treatment for mpox was available in Japan, and data on the clinical course and virological changes during tecovirimat treatment were limited. METHODS: We conducted a prospective multicenter observational case series of patients with PCR-confirmed mpox between June 28, 2022, and March 31, 2025, to describe the clinical course and safety of a 14-day course of tecovirimat. Participants could choose treatment or no treatment. RESULTS: All 31 participants received tecovirimat; 24 completed a single 14-day course, and 2 received multiple courses plus vaccinia immune globulin. The median age was 39 years (range, 21-74), and all were men. Nineteen (61.3%) were people living with HIV, with a median CD4 count of 531 cells/μL (range, 50-830), including three with CD4 counts <200 cells/μL. Severe mpox occurred in 23 participants (74.2%), three of whom (9.7%) had risk factors for severe disease, all due to CD4 < 200 cells/μL. Skin lesions were present in 30 participants. At day 14, skin PCR results were available for 23 participants; 16 (69.6%) were negative. No deaths occurred within 14 or 30 days, although one patient with advanced HIV infection died following prolonged hospitalization. Adverse events occurred in 2 participants and were considered unrelated to treatment. DISCUSSION: This prospective multicenter case series describes the clinical course of mpox and temporal dynamics of skin PCR negativity in patients receiving tecovirimat in real-world practice.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
At the time of this study, no approved treatment for mpox was available in Japan, and data on the clinical course and virological changes during tecovirimat treatment were limited.
Design and population
We conducted a prospective multicenter observational case series of patients with PCR-confirmed mpox between June 28, 2022, and March 31, 2025, to describe the clinical course and safety of a 14-day course of tecovirimat. Participants could choose treatment or no treatment.
Core methods
At day 14, skin PCR results were available for 23 participants; 16 (69.6%) were negative.
Main results
All 31 participants received tecovirimat; 24 completed a single 14-day course, and 2 received multiple courses plus vaccinia immune globulin. The median age was 39 years (range, 21-74), and all were men. Nineteen (61.3%) were people living with HIV, with a median CD4 count of 531 cells/μL (range, 50-830), including three with CD4 counts <200 cells/μL. Severe mpox occurred in 23 participants (74.2%), three of whom (9.7%) had risk factors for severe disease, all due to CD4 < 200 cells/μL. Skin lesions were present in 30 participants. No deaths occurred within 14 or 30 days, although one patient with advanced HIV infection died following prolonged hospitalization. Adverse events occurred in 2 participants and were considered unrelated to treatment.
Interpretation and novelty
This prospective multicenter case series describes the clinical course of mpox and temporal dynamics of skin PCR negativity in patients receiving tecovirimat in real-world practice.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide real-world evidence on the clinical course and virologic dynamics of mpox during tecovirimat treatment, which can inform treatment guidelines and surveillance strategies in similar settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was a prospective multicenter observational case series with a small sample size and limited follow-up period. The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational design and potential selection bias.

埃塞俄比亚中部地区公共卫生设施中医护人员对人类猴痘病毒感染重新出现的知识和态度:一项在线横断面研究

Knowledge and Attitudes of Healthcare Workers Toward the Re‐Emergence of Human Monkeypox Virus Infection in Public Health Facilities in the Central Ethiopia Region: An Online Cross‐Sectional Study
作者： Yilma Markos Larebo, Zerfework Debebe Argago, Markos Selamu Jifar, Abebe Alemu Anshebo, Sujit Kumar Behera, Natarajan Gopalan
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人猴痘(猴痘)是非洲重新出现的一种人畜共患病毒性疾病,尽管埃塞俄比亚最近有报道,但没有研究评估埃塞俄比亚中部地区医护人员的知识和态度。
设计与对象
From June 16 to 28, 2025, 419 healthcare workers were chosen at random for an online cross-sectional study in public health facilities in the Central Ethiopia Region.
核心方法
使用通过 Google Forms 分发的自填问卷收集数据,并使用 SPSS 26 版进行统计分析,包括多变量逻辑回归以确定与知识和态度相关的因素。
主要结果
在 419 名参与者中,46.8%(95% CI:42.0–51.7)具有良好的知识,而 43.4%(95% CI:39.1–48.0)对 猴痘 表现出积极态度。
解释与创新
作者解释说,医护人员对 猴痘 的了解水平较低且态度积极,这凸显了有针对性的干预措施的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
为了提高埃塞俄比亚对 猴痘 的准备和应对能力,加强继续专业教育、有针对性的培训并提高医护人员的认识非常重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于特定地区和时间范围,由于横截面设计和在线调查中潜在的自我选择偏差,证据强度中等。

Knowledge and Attitudes of Healthcare Workers Toward the Re‐Emergence of Human Monkeypox Virus Infection in Public Health Facilities in the Central Ethiopia Region: An Online Cross‐Sectional Study

Authors: Yilma Markos Larebo, Zerfework Debebe Argago, Markos Selamu Jifar, Abebe Alemu Anshebo, Sujit Kumar Behera, Natarajan Gopalan
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Background and Aims Human monkeypox (mpox) is a re‐emerging zoonotic viral disease in Africa. Despite recent reports in Ethiopia, no studies have assessed healthcare workers' (HCWs) knowledge and attitudes in the Central Ethiopia Region. This study aimed to determine HCWs knowledge and attitudes toward the re‐emergence of mpox in public health facilities. Methods From June 16 to 28, 2025, 419 HCWs were chosen at random for an online cross‐sectional study. The data were gathered using a self‐administered questionnaire distributed through Google Forms. We used SPSS version 26 for the statistical analysis. Multivariable logistic regression was used to identify factors associated with knowledge and attitudes. Adjusted odds ratios (AORs) with 95% confidence intervals (CIs) were used to determine statistical significance. Results Of the 419 participants, 46.8% (95% CI: 42.0–51.7) had good knowledge, while 43.4% (95% CI: 39.1–48.0) showed positive attitudes. Good knowledge were significantly positively associated with a master's degree or higher (AOR = 3.46; 95% CI: 1.60–7.47), health officer (AOR = 7.00; 95% CI: 3.20–15.32) and pharmacist (AOR = 2.68; 95% CI: 1.17–6.13), low (AOR = 5.15; 95% CI: 2.02–13.10) and medium monthly income (AOR = 2.35; 95% CI: 1.32–4.20), and positive attitude (AOR = 2.11; 95% CI: 1.25–3.59), in contrast significantly negatively associated with work experience (5–10 years) (AOR = 0.13; 95% CI: 0.07–0.24). Positive attitudes were significantly positively associated with medical doctor (AOR = 4.78; 95% CI: 2.39–9.55), and information about mpox during medical education (AOR = 3.29; 95% CI: 1.91–5.67), in contrast significantly negatively associated with female sex (AOR = 0.18; 95% CI: 0.08–0.41), age ≥ 32 years (AOR = 0.45; 95% CI: 0.28–0.73), and lack of current information access (AOR = 0.47; 95% CI: 0.26–0.88). Conclusion HCWs had low levels of knowledge and positive attitudes toward mpox. To get better at being ready for and responding to mpox in Ethiopia, it is important to strengthen continuing professional education, target training, and raise awareness.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Human monkeypox (mpox) is a re-emerging zoonotic viral disease in Africa, and despite recent reports in Ethiopia, no studies have assessed healthcare workers' knowledge and attitudes in the Central Ethiopia Region.
Design and population
From June 16 to 28, 2025, 419 healthcare workers were chosen at random for an online cross-sectional study in public health facilities in the Central Ethiopia Region.
Core methods
The data were gathered using a self-administered questionnaire distributed through Google Forms, and SPSS version 26 was used for statistical analysis, including multivariable logistic regression to identify factors associated with knowledge and attitudes.
Main results
Of the 419 participants, 46.8% (95% CI: 42.0–51.7) had good knowledge, while 43.4% (95% CI: 39.1–48.0) showed positive attitudes toward mpox.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that healthcare workers had low levels of knowledge and positive attitudes toward mpox, highlighting a need for targeted interventions.
Scientific and public-health significance
To improve preparedness and response to mpox in Ethiopia, it is important to strengthen continuing professional education, target training, and raise awareness among healthcare workers.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited to a specific region and time frame, and the evidence strength is moderate due to the cross-sectional design and potential self-selection bias in the online survey.

基因组 修饰的非复制型痘苗病毒天坛株的复制和免疫原性增强,可为小鼠提供强大的低剂量保护

Enhanced replication and immunogenicity of a genome-modified non-replicating vaccinia virus Tiantan strain for robust low-dose protection in mice
作者： Jiao Ren, Shiyuan Liu, Yuan Hang, Yiyi Wu, Zhihao Zhao, Yawei Wang, Baoying Huang, Wenling Wang, 田厚文, Wenjie Tan
摘要中文翻译摘要 基于天坛株的非复制痘苗病毒(NTV)在 疫苗接种 和基因治疗方面具有重要的应用前景,并且最近作为一种新型且更安全的 疫苗 抗猴痘候选药物进入临床试验。然而,仍然需要优化,特别是在其生产能力和免疫原性方面。在这项研究中,通过使用CRISPR/Cas9介导的基因编辑和同源重组修饰非复制病毒骨架中的F1L和C7L基因,构建了重组病毒。由此产生的构建体,命名为 NTV-ΔF1L-C7L,在 疫苗 生产细胞系中表现出显着增强的复制,与亲本 NTV 相比,MRC-5 细胞中的病毒产量增加了 680 倍以上。其对小鼠的致病力显着降低,与痘苗病毒天坛株(VTT)的致病力相比下降了10倍以上。两次肌肉注射后,NTV-ΔF1L-C7L 引发高滴度的 正痘病毒 特异性 IgG 和针对牛痘和猴痘病毒的中和抗体,以及表现出 Th1 偏好的强大细胞 免疫应答,其明显强于亲本 NTV 或 VTT 疫苗接种 诱导的效果。在用低剂量 (10 3 PFU) 或单剂量 (105 PFU) NTV-ΔF1L-C7L 免疫的小鼠中,获得了针对牛痘病毒西储株致命攻击的完全保护 (100%),与 VTT 所提供的保护相当。这些发现表明,NTV-ΔF1L-C7L 结合了高安全性、增强的复制性和免疫原性,支持其作为新型 疫苗 媒介 的价值及其在控制当前全球猴痘 暴发 方面的潜在应用。重要性 高度衰减的 NTV 表现出更高的安全性;然而,其生产能力和免疫原性需要针对临床应用进行优化。在这项研究中,通过靶向删除 F1L 并将 C7L 插入 NTV 主链,开发了一种新型重组病毒 NTV-ΔF1L-C7L。这种衰减的实时 疫苗、NTV-ΔF1L-C7L 展示了几个显着的优势。其强大的体外复制支持大规模 疫苗 生产的可扩展性。它在小鼠中表现出强大的免疫保护功效,同时保持高安全性,表现出体内致病性降低,并诱导针对牛痘和猴痘病毒的强大体液和细胞 免疫应答。这些 免疫应答 在低剂量或单剂量免疫接种时提供针对致命 VACV 挑战的完整保护。这些发现为 NTV-ΔF1L-C7L 作为下一代基于 VACV 的 媒介 或针对 猴痘 的候选 疫苗 的进一步开发奠定了坚实的实验基础。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
基于天坛株的非复制痘苗病毒(NTV)在疫苗接种和基因治疗方面具有重要的应用前景,并且最近作为新型、更安全的疫苗抗猴痘候选药物进入临床试验;然而,仍然需要优化,特别是在其生产能力和免疫原性方面。
设计与对象
在这项研究中,通过使用CRISPR/Cas9介导的基因编辑和同源重组修饰非复制病毒骨架中的F1L和C7L基因,构建了重组病毒。
核心方法
评估所得构建体(命名为 NTV-ΔF1L-C7L)在 疫苗 生产细胞系中的复制、小鼠中的致病性以及肌内免疫后的免疫原性。
主要结果
NTV-ΔF1L-C7L 在 疫苗 生产细胞系中表现出显着增强的复制,与亲本 NTV 相比,MRC-5 细胞中的病毒产量增加了 680 倍以上;其对小鼠的致病力显着降低,与痘苗病毒天坛株(VTT)的致病力相比下降10倍以上;两次肌肉注射后,NTV-ΔF1L-C7L 引发高滴度的 正痘病毒 特异性 IgG 和针对牛痘和猴痘病毒的中和抗体,以及表现出 Th1 偏好的强大细胞 免疫应答,其明显强于亲本 NTV 或 VTT 疫苗接种 诱导的效果。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,NTV-ΔF1L-C7L 结合了高安全性、增强的复制性和免疫原性,支持其作为新型 疫苗 媒介 的价值及其在控制当前全球猴痘 暴发 方面的潜在应用。
科研与公卫意义
NTV-ΔF1L-C7L 表现出几个显着的优势,包括强大的体外复制支持大规模 疫苗 生产的可扩展性、在保持高安全裕度的同时对小鼠具有强大的免疫保护功效,以及在低剂量或单剂量免疫时完全防止致命的 VACV 攻击。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的具体局限性;然而,证据强度很高,因为该研究表明复制、致病性和免疫原性显着改善,并能完全保护小鼠免受致命攻击。

Enhanced replication and immunogenicity of a genome-modified non-replicating vaccinia virus Tiantan strain for robust low-dose protection in mice

Authors: Jiao Ren, Shiyuan Liu, Yuan Hang, Yiyi Wu, Zhihao Zhao, Yawei Wang, Baoying Huang, Wenling Wang, 田厚文, Wenjie Tan
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT A non-replicating Tiantan strain-based vaccinia virus (NTV) holds significant application prospects for vaccination and gene therapy and has recently entered clinical trials as a novel and safer vaccine candidate against monkeypox. However, optimization is still required, particularly regarding its production capacity and immunogenicity. In this study, a recombinant virus was constructed by modifying the F1L and C7L genes in a non-replicating viral backbone using CRISPR/Cas9-mediated gene editing and homologous recombination. The resulting construct, designated NTV-ΔF1L-C7L, exhibited significantly enhanced replication in vaccine production cell lines, with viral yields increasing by more than 680-fold in MRC-5 cells compared to those of the parental NTV. Its pathogenicity in mice was significantly reduced, showing more than a 10-fold decrease compared to the pathogenicity of the vaccinia virus Tiantan strain (VTT). Following two intramuscular doses, NTV-ΔF1L-C7L elicited high titers of orthopoxvirus-specific IgG and neutralizing antibodies against vaccinia and monkeypox viruses, along with a robust cellular immune response exhibiting a Th1 bias, which was significantly stronger than that induced by either parental NTV or VTT vaccination. Complete protection (100%) against a lethal challenge with the vaccinia virus Western Reserve strain was achieved in mice immunized with either a low dose (10 3 PFU) or a single dose (105 PFU) of NTV-ΔF1L-C7L, comparable to that conferred by VTT. These findings demonstrate that NTV-ΔF1L-C7L combines high safety with enhanced replication and immunogenicity, supporting its value as a novel vaccine vector and its potential application in controlling the current global monkeypox outbreak. IMPORTANCE A highly attenuated NTV exhibits an improved safety profile; however, its production capacity and immunogenicity require optimization for clinical application. In this study, a novel recombinant virus, NTV-ΔF1L-C7L, was developed by targeting the deletion of F1L and the insertion of C7L into the NTV backbone. This attenuated live vaccine, NTV-ΔF1L-C7L, demonstrates several significant advantages. Its robust in vitro replication supports scalability for large-scale vaccine production. It has demonstrated strong immunoprotective efficacy in mice while maintaining a high safety margin, exhibiting reduced pathogenicity in vivo , and inducing robust humoral and cellular immune responses against vaccinia and monkeypox virus. These immune responses confer complete protection against lethal VACV challenge at low-dose or single-dose immunization. These findings establish a solid experimental foundation for the further development of NTV-ΔF1L-C7L as a next-generation VACV-based vector or a candidate vaccine against mpox.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
A non-replicating Tiantan strain-based vaccinia virus (NTV) holds significant application prospects for vaccination and gene therapy and has recently entered clinical trials as a novel and safer vaccine candidate against monkeypox; however, optimization is still required, particularly regarding its production capacity and immunogenicity.
Design and population
In this study, a recombinant virus was constructed by modifying the F1L and C7L genes in a non-replicating viral backbone using CRISPR/Cas9-mediated gene editing and homologous recombination.
Core methods
The resulting construct, designated NTV-ΔF1L-C7L, was evaluated for replication in vaccine production cell lines, pathogenicity in mice, and immunogenicity following intramuscular immunization.
Main results
NTV-ΔF1L-C7L exhibited significantly enhanced replication in vaccine production cell lines, with viral yields increasing by more than 680-fold in MRC-5 cells compared to those of the parental NTV; its pathogenicity in mice was significantly reduced, showing more than a 10-fold decrease compared to the pathogenicity of the vaccinia virus Tiantan strain (VTT); following two intramuscular doses, NTV-ΔF1L-C7L elicited high titers of orthopoxvirus-specific IgG and neutralizing antibodies against vaccinia and monkeypox viruses, along with a robust cellular immune response exhibiting a Th1 bias, which was significantly stronger than that induced by either parental NTV or VTT vaccination.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings demonstrate that NTV-ΔF1L-C7L combines high safety with enhanced replication and immunogenicity, supporting its value as a novel vaccine vector and its potential application in controlling the current global monkeypox outbreak.
Scientific and public-health significance
NTV-ΔF1L-C7L demonstrates several significant advantages, including robust in vitro replication supporting scalability for large-scale vaccine production, strong immunoprotective efficacy in mice while maintaining a high safety margin, and complete protection against lethal VACV challenge at low-dose or single-dose immunization.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study; however, the evidence strength is high as the study demonstrates significant improvements in replication, pathogenicity, and immunogenicity with complete protection against lethal challenge in mice.

进化枝 II 猴痘 的 特考韦瑞 治疗:随机、双盲、3 期试验的病毒学分析。

Tecovirimat treatment of clade II mpox: a virological analysis of a randomised, double-blind, phase 3 trial.
作者： Dariia Vyshenska, Jonathan C Reed, Pavitra Roychoudhury, Lu Zheng, Maxine Olefsky, Justin Ritz, Caitlyn McCarthy, Pooja T Saha, Linhui Hao, Margaret G Mills
摘要中文翻译背景:猴痘病毒(MPXV)已成为全球公共卫生威胁。特考韦瑞 的三项 3 期试验未显示出实质性的临床益处。我们报告了全球推进临床治疗 (ACTG) A5418-特考韦瑞 人类 猴痘 的研究 (A5418-STOMP) 的病毒分离和测序结果。方法:ACTG A5418-STOMP 是一项 3 期随机、安慰剂对照、双盲试验 (NCT05534984),另外还有一个开放标签组,招募儿童、孕妇、严重免疫抑制或严重疾病参与者。第 1 天(基线)、第 8 天和第 15 天指数病变皮肤拭子用于病毒分离。对第 57 天的指数和新皮损以及直肠、口腔、阴道、血液和尿液样本进行 F13L 基因的深度测序。使用预定义的耐药相关 突变 (RAM) 列表将参与者分类为无 RAM、传播 RAM 或 治疗 出现 RAM。使用费舍尔精确检验比较比例;使用 Mann-Whitney U 检验比较持续时间归一化的 log10 MPXV DNA 拷贝。P 值在适用的情况下使用 Benjamini-Hochberg 校正进行调整。结果:在第 1 天使用第 8 天可用拭子检测呈培养阳性的参与者中,随机 特考韦瑞 组中 53 名参与者中的 5 名 (9%)、安慰剂组中 25 名参与者中的 3 名 (12%) 和开放标签组中 67 名参与者中的 8 名 (12%) 在第 8 天时培养呈阳性 (p=0·71)。在拥有第一天和纵向测序数据的参与者中,随机 特考韦瑞 组的 54 名参与者中有 1 名 (2%) 观察到了 治疗 突发耐药性,安慰剂组的 29 名参与者中有 0 名观察到了 治疗 突发耐药性,而开放标签组的 92 名参与者中有 6 名 (65%) 观察到了 治疗 突发耐药性。与没有耐药性的 治疗 参与者相比,具有 治疗 耐药性的参与者在第 57 天时具有显着更高的纵向病毒载量 (p=0·0014) 和较低的临床解决率 (28·6% vs 94·7%;p=3·8 × 10-5)。携带 治疗 突变体的 7 名个体中,有 6 名感染了 HIV;所有五个可用基线 CD4+ T 细胞计数的记录值均低于每 μL 80 个细胞。在 23 名免疫功能低下的 HIV 感染者中,有 5 名 (22%) 出现了 治疗 紧急 RAM,其临床解决率明显低于未出现 RAM 的患者(5 名感染者中的 1 名 [20%] vs 18 名中的 17 名 [94%];p=0·0027)。解释:虽然总体而言,治疗 出现的对 特考韦瑞 的耐药性很少见,但在晚期 HIV 患者和细胞免疫严重受损的参与者中更常见,导致病毒载量持续较高和临床恢复延迟。特考韦瑞 治疗 猴痘 感染并没有降低从皮肤拭子中分离 MPXV 的频率。需要新的治疗方案来解决 猴痘 感染。资助:在全球范围内推进临床治疗以及美国国立卫生研究院国家过敏和传染病研究所。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
猴痘病毒 (MPXV) 已成为全球公共卫生威胁,需要评估 特考韦瑞 治疗 针对进化枝 II 猴痘 的功效。
设计与对象
ACTG A5418-STOMP 是一项 3 期随机、安慰剂对照、双盲试验 (NCT05534984),另外还有一个开放标签组,招募儿童、孕妇、严重免疫抑制或严重疾病参与者。
核心方法
第1天(基线)、第8天和第15天指数病变皮肤拭子用于病毒分离;在第 57 天对指数和新皮损以及直肠、口腔、阴道、血液和尿液样本进行 F13L 基因深度测序;使用 Fisher 精确检验比较比例,使用 Mann-Whitney U 检验比较持续时间标准化 log10 MPXV DNA 拷贝。
主要结果
在第1天培养阳性且有第8天拭子可用的参与者中,随机分组特考韦瑞组的53名参与者中有5名(9%)、安慰剂组的25名参与者中有3名(12%)、开放标签组的67名参与者中有8名(12%)在第8天培养阳性(p=0·71);在随机分组特考韦瑞组的54名参与者中有1名(2%)出现治疗耐药性,安慰剂组的29名参与者中有0名,开放标签组的92名参与者中有6名(65%)出现治疗耐药性;出现治疗耐药性的参与者纵向病毒载量显著更高(p=0·0014),且到第57天的临床缓解率较低(28·6% vs 94·7%;p=3·8 × 10-5),无耐药性的参与者。
解释与创新
尽管 治疗 对 特考韦瑞 的耐药性总体上很少见,但在患有晚期 HIV 且细胞免疫严重受损的参与者中更常见,导致病毒载量持续较高并延迟临床恢复;特考韦瑞 治疗 猴痘 感染并没有降低从皮肤拭子中分离 MPXV 的频率。
科研与公卫意义
需要新的治疗方案来解决 猴痘 感染,特别是对于免疫功能低下的 HIV 感染者,他们可能会产生 治疗 突发耐药性并经历较差的临床结果。
局限与证据强度
由于该研究的随机设计和全面的病毒学分析,证据强度中等,但其局限性包括某些亚组的样本量较小以及开放标签组中可能存在混杂因素。

Tecovirimat treatment of clade II mpox: a virological analysis of a randomised, double-blind, phase 3 trial.

Authors: Dariia Vyshenska, Jonathan C Reed, Pavitra Roychoudhury, Lu Zheng, Maxine Olefsky, Justin Ritz, Caitlyn McCarthy, Pooja T Saha, Linhui Hao, Margaret G Mills
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Monkeypox virus (MPXV) has emerged as a global public health threat. Three phase 3 trials of tecovirimat showed no substantial clinical benefit. We report virus isolation and sequencing results from the Advancing Clinical Therapeutics Globally (ACTG) A5418-The Study of Tecovirimat for Human Mpox (A5418-STOMP). METHODS: ACTG A5418-STOMP was a phase 3 randomised, placebo-controlled, double-blind trial (NCT05534984) with an additional open-label arm enrolling paediatric, pregnant, severely immunosuppressed, or severe-disease participants. Day 1 (baseline), day 8, and day 15 index lesion skin swabs were used for virus isolation. Deep sequencing of the F13L gene was performed on index and new skin lesions, and on rectal, oral, vaginal, blood, and urine specimens through day 57. A predefined list of resistance-associated mutations (RAMs) was used to classify participants as having no RAMs, transmitted RAMs, or treatment-emergent RAMs. Proportions were compared using Fisher's exact tests; duration-normalised log10 MPXV DNA copies were compared using Mann-Whitney U tests. P values were adjusted using Benjamini-Hochberg corrections where applicable. FINDINGS: Among participants who were culture-positive on day 1 with available day 8 swabs, five (9%) of 53 in the randomised tecovirimat arm, three (12%) of 25 in the placebo arm, and eight (12%) of 67 in the open-label arm were culture-positive on day 8 (p=0·71). Among participants with day 1 and longitudinal sequencing data available, treatment-emergent resistance was observed in one (2%) of 54 of the randomised tecovirimat arm, zero of 29 of the placebo arm, and six (65%) of 92 of the open-label arm. Participants with treatment-emergent resistance had significantly higher longitudinal viral loads (p=0·0014) and lower clinical resolution rates by day 57 (28·6% vs 94·7%; p=3·8 × 10-5) than those without resistance mutations. Six of seven individuals with treatment-emergent variants were living with HIV; all five with available baseline CD4+ T-cell counts recorded values below 80 cells per μL. Among 23 immunocompromised participants living with HIV, five (22%) developed treatment-emergent RAMs, with markedly lower clinical resolution rates than those who did not (one [20%] of five vs 17 [94%] of 18; p=0·0027). INTERPRETATION: Although treatment-emergent resistance to tecovirimat was rare overall, it was observed more often in participants living with advanced HIV and profoundly impaired cellular immunity, leading to persistently higher viral loads and delayed clinical recovery. Tecovirimat therapy of mpox infection did not reduce the frequency of MPXV isolation from skin swabs. Novel therapeutic options are needed to address mpox infection. FUNDING: Advancing Clinical Therapeutics Globally and the National Institute of Allergy and Infectious Diseases, US National Institutes of Health.

Question and background
Monkeypox virus (MPXV) has emerged as a global public health threat, necessitating evaluation of tecovirimat treatment efficacy against clade II mpox.
Design and population
ACTG A5418-STOMP was a phase 3 randomised, placebo-controlled, double-blind trial (NCT05534984) with an additional open-label arm enrolling paediatric, pregnant, severely immunosuppressed, or severe-disease participants.
Core methods
Day 1 (baseline), day 8, and day 15 index lesion skin swabs were used for virus isolation; deep sequencing of the F13L gene was performed on index and new skin lesions, and on rectal, oral, vaginal, blood, and urine specimens through day 57; proportions were compared using Fisher's exact tests and duration-normalised log10 MPXV DNA copies using Mann-Whitney U tests.
Main results
Among participants who were culture-positive on day 1 with available day 8 swabs, five (9%) of 53 in the randomised tecovirimat arm, three (12%) of 25 in the placebo arm, and eight (12%) of 67 in the open-label arm were culture-positive on day 8 (p=0·71); treatment-emergent resistance was observed in one (2%) of 54 of the randomised tecovirimat arm, zero of 29 of the placebo arm, and six (65%) of 92 of the open-label arm; participants with treatment-emergent resistance had significantly higher longitudinal viral loads (p=0·0014) and lower clinical resolution rates by day 57 (28·6% vs 94·7%; p=3·8 × 10-5) than those without resistance mutations.
Interpretation and novelty
Although treatment-emergent resistance to tecovirimat was rare overall, it was observed more often in participants living with advanced HIV and profoundly impaired cellular immunity, leading to persistently higher viral loads and delayed clinical recovery; tecovirimat therapy of mpox infection did not reduce the frequency of MPXV isolation from skin swabs.
Scientific and public-health significance
Novel therapeutic options are needed to address mpox infection, particularly for immunocompromised individuals living with HIV who may develop treatment-emergent resistance and experience poorer clinical outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the study's randomised design and comprehensive virological analysis, but limitations include the small sample size for certain subgroups and the potential for confounding factors in the open-label arm.

下一代 猴痘 疫苗:通过 PD-1 检查点调制增强基于 MVA 的免疫力 2309232

Next-Generation Mpox Vaccine: Enhancing MVA-Based Immunity Through PD-1 Checkpoint Modulation 2309232
作者： Anoli Karunathilake, Crystal Lawson, Reem Miah, Sreenivasa Oruganti, Mary J. Hauser, J D Burleson, Heather Koehler
摘要中文翻译摘要 介绍 2022 猴痘 暴发 暴露了 正痘病毒 疫苗 耐久性方面的差距,因为获得许可的两剂改良痘苗安卡拉 (MVA) 方案仅提供中等且逐渐减弱的保护。持久的 抗病毒药物 免疫需要协调的体液反应和 CD8+ T 细胞反应,其中 CD8+ T 细胞在控制病毒复制位点的感染细胞方面发挥着重要作用。然而,当前的 疫苗 策略不能完全维持这些细胞反应。为了解决这一限制,MVA-X被设计为基于MVA的疫苗,其中PD-1拮抗剂肽直接在病毒基因组内编码,从而在抗原呈递过程中实现局部检查点调节。方法 年龄和性别匹配的 C57BL/6 小鼠接受制剂缓冲液、单剂量 MVA、两剂量 MVA 或单剂量 MVA-X。小鼠在接种后第 55、90 或 150 天接受致命痘苗病毒 (VACV-WR) 攻击。通过生存、肺病毒滴度和 基因组 负荷、肺组织病理学和免疫组织化学、血清中和和结合抗体测定以及病毒特异性 CD8+ T 细胞反应来评估 疫苗 功效和 免疫应答。结果 单剂 MVA-X 在所有攻击时间点均可实现完全存活,相当于或超过两剂 MVA 保护。在攻击后第 90 天,MVA-X 疫苗接种 导致肺部病毒滴度和 基因组 拷贝降低 > 97%,保留肺泡结构和局部细支气管周围免疫浸润,与有效的组织水平病毒控制一致。尽管结合抗体反应下降,但动物仍然受到保护,这与在引发后 150 天可检测到的持续病毒特异性 CD8+ T 细胞反应一致。结论 这些发现表明,MVA-X 在单次免疫后支持持久的细胞 免疫应答。这种 疫苗 内在检查点调节策略提供了一个模块化框架,具有更广泛的适用性,可增强病毒 疫苗 开发中的细胞免疫。资金来源 GeoVax,Inc 主题类别 疫苗 和免疫疗法 (VAC)
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
2022 年 猴痘 暴发 暴露了 正痘病毒 疫苗 耐久性方面的差距,因为获得许可的两剂改良痘苗安卡拉 (MVA) 方案仅提供中等且逐渐减弱的保护。
设计与对象
年龄和性别匹配的 C57BL/6 小鼠接受制剂缓冲液、单剂量 MVA、两剂量 MVA 或单剂量 MVA-X,并在免疫后第 55、90 或 150 天用致死痘苗病毒 (VACV-WR) 进行攻击。
核心方法
通过生存、肺病毒滴度和 基因组 负荷、肺组织病理学和免疫组织化学、血清中和和结合抗体测定以及病毒特异性 CD8+ T 细胞反应来评估 疫苗 功效和 免疫应答。
主要结果
单剂 MVA-X 在所有攻击时间点均能实现完全存活,相当于或超过两剂 MVA 保护,并导致攻击后第 90 天肺部病毒滴度和 基因组 拷贝数降低 >97%。
解释与创新
这些研究结果表明,MVA-X 在一次免疫后支持持久的细胞 免疫应答,提供了一个具有更广泛适用性的模块化框架,用于增强病毒 疫苗 开发中的细胞免疫。
科研与公卫意义
这种 疫苗 内在检查点调制策略可以提高 猴痘 疫苗 的耐久性并减少所需的剂量数量,从而可能增强 暴发 的控制并减少 疫苗接种 活动中的后勤负担。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究是在痘苗病毒攻击的小鼠模型中进行的,这可能无法完全转化为人类 猴痘 感染或自然疾病进展。

Next-Generation Mpox Vaccine: Enhancing MVA-Based Immunity Through PD-1 Checkpoint Modulation 2309232

Authors: Anoli Karunathilake, Crystal Lawson, Reem Miah, Sreenivasa Oruganti, Mary J. Hauser, J D Burleson, Heather Koehler
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction The 2022 Mpox outbreak exposed gaps in orthopoxvirus vaccine durability, as the licensed two-dose Modified Vaccinia Ankara (MVA) regimen provides only moderate and waning protection. Durable antiviral immunity requires coordinated humoral and CD8+ T-cell responses, with CD8+ T cells playing an important role in controlling infected cells at sites of viral replication. However, current vaccine strategies incompletely sustain these cellular responses. To address this limitation, MVA-X was engineered as an MVA-based vaccine in which a PD-1 antagonist peptide is encoded directly within the viral genome, enabling localized checkpoint modulation during antigen presentation. Methods Age- and sex-matched C57BL/6 mice received formulation buffer, single-dose MVA, two-dose MVA, or single-dose MVA-X. Mice were challenged with lethal vaccinia virus (VACV-WR) at days 55, 90, or 150 post-prime. Vaccine efficacy and immune responses were evaluated by survival, lung viral titers and genome burden, lung histopathology and immunohistochemistry, serum neutralization and binding antibody assays, and virus-specific CD8+ T-cell responses. Results A single dose of MVA-X conferred complete survival at all challenge time points, matching or exceeding two doses of MVA protection. At day 90 post-challenge, MVA-X vaccination resulted in > 97% reductions in lung viral titers and genome copies, preservation of alveolar architecture, and localized peribronchiolar immune infiltrates, consistent with efficient tissue-level viral control. Despite declining binding antibody responses, animals remained protected, consistent with sustained virus-specific CD8+ T-cell responses detectable through day 150 post-prime. Conclusion These findings show that MVA-X supports durable cellular immune responses following a single immunization. This vaccine-intrinsic checkpoint modulation strategy offers a modular framework with broader applicability for enhancing cellular immunity in viral vaccine development. Funding Source GeoVax,Inc Topic Categories Vaccines and Immunotherapy (VAC)
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The 2022 Mpox outbreak exposed gaps in orthopoxvirus vaccine durability, as the licensed two-dose Modified Vaccinia Ankara (MVA) regimen provides only moderate and waning protection.
Design and population
Age- and sex-matched C57BL/6 mice received formulation buffer, single-dose MVA, two-dose MVA, or single-dose MVA-X and were challenged with lethal vaccinia virus (VACV-WR) at days 55, 90, or 150 post-prime.
Core methods
Vaccine efficacy and immune responses were evaluated by survival, lung viral titers and genome burden, lung histopathology and immunohistochemistry, serum neutralization and binding antibody assays, and virus-specific CD8+ T-cell responses.
Main results
A single dose of MVA-X conferred complete survival at all challenge time points, matching or exceeding two doses of MVA protection, and resulted in >97% reductions in lung viral titers and genome copies at day 90 post-challenge.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings show that MVA-X supports durable cellular immune responses following a single immunization, offering a modular framework with broader applicability for enhancing cellular immunity in viral vaccine development.
Scientific and public-health significance
This vaccine-intrinsic checkpoint modulation strategy could improve mpox vaccine durability and reduce the number of doses required, potentially enhancing outbreak control and reducing logistical burdens in vaccination campaigns.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate as the study was conducted in a mouse model with vaccinia virus challenge, which may not fully translate to human mpox infection or natural disease progression.

痘病毒门复合体的原位结构。

In situ structure of the poxvirus portal complex.
作者： Thomas Calcraft, Miguel Hernandez-Gonzalez, Michael Way, Peter B. Rosenthal, Miguel Hernández‐González, Miguel Hernández-González
摘要中文翻译摘要 痘病毒是一个大型、复杂的双链 DNA 病毒家族,其中包括人类病原体,例如天花(天花的病因)和猴痘。最近的 猴痘 的 暴发 强调需要更好地了解痘病毒生物学 1,2。痘病毒组装是一个保守的过程,涉及在成熟病毒的膜内形成双凹核心 3,4。在这里,我们使用冷冻电子断层扫描结合亚断层平均和结构预测来确定痘苗病毒(典型的痘病毒)中门静脉复合体(跨越核心壁的孔)的结构和组成。六聚体复合物由 E8、E6 和 L3 蛋白组成,这些蛋白在痘病毒中是保守的,并且对于感染建立过程中 mRNA 的释放至关重要 5-7。E6 也是病毒组装 8-10 所必需的,它形成门户的中央室并与周围的核心壁相互作用。E8 的六聚体附着在 E6 的外侧。L3 是 TRIM5α 介导的限制性靶标 11,以二聚体的六聚体形式与内侧结合。此外,基因组从核心12释放所需的病毒解旋酶D5在感染期间通过对接至门复合体的外部E8边缘与细胞质核心相关联。我们认为门静脉复合物代表了抗痘病毒疗法开发的一个有吸引力的靶点。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
痘病毒,包括天花和猴痘等人类病原体,仍然是一个公共卫生问题,最近的 猴痘 暴发 突显了对痘病毒生物学的理解空白,这可以为治疗开发提供信息。
设计与对象
该研究使用冷冻电子断层扫描结合亚断层图平均和结构预测来确定牛痘病毒(一种典型的痘病毒)中门静脉复合物的结构和组成。
核心方法
冷冻电子断层扫描结合亚断层平均和结构预测用于解析门静脉复合物,并通过结构和相互作用分析鉴定其蛋白质组成。
主要结果
门静脉复合体是由E8、E6和L3蛋白组成的六聚体结构,其中E6形成中央室并与核心壁相互作用,E8附着在外部,L3作为六聚体二聚体在内部结合。
解释与创新
作者提出,对于 mRNA 释放和病毒组装至关重要的门户复合体代表了抗痘病毒治疗的潜在靶点,强调了其结构保守和功能作用。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,针对门户复合体可以通过破坏基本的组装和感染过程来为针对痘病毒(包括 猴痘)的 抗病毒药物 策略的开发提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于牛痘病毒(一种相关的痘病毒),由于依赖结构推论且缺乏 猴痘病毒 中的直接功能验证,因此证据强度中等。

In situ structure of the poxvirus portal complex.

Authors: Thomas Calcraft, Miguel Hernandez-Gonzalez, Michael Way, Peter B. Rosenthal, Miguel Hernández‐González, Miguel Hernández-González
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Poxviruses are a family of large, complex double-stranded DNA viruses that includes human pathogens such as variola—the cause of smallpox—and monkeypox. Recent outbreaks of mpox underscore the need for a better understanding of poxvirus biology 1,2 . Poxvirus assembly is a conserved process that involves the formation of a biconcave core inside the membrane of the maturing virus 3,4 . Here we use cryo-electron tomography combined with subtomogram averaging and structure prediction to determine the structure and composition of the portal complex—a pore that spans the core wall—in vaccinia virus, the prototypical poxvirus. The hexameric complex consists of the E8, E6 and L3 proteins, which are conserved across poxviruses and essential for mRNA release during the establishment of infection 5–7 . E6, which is also required for virus assembly 8–10 , forms the central chamber of the portal and interacts with the surrounding core wall. A hexamer of E8 attaches to the exterior side of E6. L3, a target of TRIM5α-mediated restriction 11 , binds as a hexamer of dimers to the interior side. Furthermore, the viral helicase D5, which is required for genome release from cores 12 , associates with cytoplasmic cores during infection by docking onto the exterior E8 rim of the portal complex. We propose that the portal complex represents an attractive target for the development of anti-poxvirus therapeutics.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Poxviruses, including human pathogens such as variola and monkeypox, remain a public-health concern, and recent mpox outbreaks highlight gaps in understanding poxvirus biology that could inform therapeutic development.
Design and population
The study used cryo-electron tomography with subtomogram averaging and structure prediction to determine the structure and composition of the portal complex in vaccinia virus, a prototypical poxvirus.
Core methods
Cryo-electron tomography combined with subtomogram averaging and structure prediction were used to resolve the portal complex, and its protein composition was identified through structural and interaction analyses.
Main results
The portal complex is a hexameric structure composed of E8, E6 and L3 proteins, with E6 forming the central chamber and interacting with the core wall, E8 attaching externally, and L3 binding internally as hexameric dimers.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors propose that the portal complex, essential for mRNA release and virus assembly, represents a potential target for anti-poxvirus therapeutics, highlighting its structural conservation and functional roles.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that targeting the portal complex could inform the development of antiviral strategies against poxviruses, including mpox, by disrupting essential assembly and infection processes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to vaccinia virus, a related poxvirus, and the evidence strength is moderate due to reliance on structural inference and the absence of direct functional validation in mpox virus.

猴痘病毒 可以抑制睾丸细胞中的病毒感应和 抗病毒药物 反应:Sirtuins 的潜在作用 2310247

Mpox virus can suppress virus sensing and antiviral Responses in testicular cells: potential role of Sirtuins 2310247
作者： Matthew Putnam, Johnathan Cornell, Saguna Verma
摘要中文翻译摘要 介绍 猴痘病毒 (MPXV) clade IIb 的全球 暴发 包括约 106,000 例,主要发生在男性中,引起了对性 传播 的严重关注。痘病毒通过阻断病原体感应和抑制 抗病毒药物 反应来逃避免疫检测。我们在此描述了人类睾丸细胞中 MPXV 感染的特征,以深入了解细胞类型特异性睾丸对感染的反应。方法 用 MPXV clade IIb (MOI 0.005) 感染人原代支持细胞 (SC) 和 Leydig (LC) 细胞。通过定量 PCR 和噬菌斑测定证实感染。使用感染后第 1 天、第 3 天和第 5 天 (dpi) 感染的 SC 和 LC 的 RNA 样本进行 RNA 测序分析。根据严格的截断值(log2 倍数变化 >|1.5|;调整 p 值 < 0.05)鉴定差异表达基因 (DEG),并使用 Ingenuity Pathway Analysis 分析病毒反应和类固醇生成途径的富集。结果 SC 和 LC 均证实有效感染,病毒滴度在 5 dpi 时达到峰值,没有明显的细胞死亡。尽管病毒复制在 1 dpi 时就很明显,但 DEG 的数量仍然很低(LC 中为 86 个,SC 中为 51 个),而两种细胞类型在 5 dpi 时均超过 3000 个。值得注意的是,典型的病毒传感、细胞死亡和炎症途径在任何时间点都没有显示任何一种细胞类型的显着激活。Sirtuin 家族基因 (SIRT) 在 3 dpi 和 5 dpi 时上调,与 抗病毒药物 反应基因(包括 DNA 病毒感应基因 IFI16)的下调相关。结论 这是首个证据表明 MPXV 可以感染关键的人类睾丸类型并抑制关键的 抗病毒药物 通路,类似于牛痘病毒。除了充当病毒后感应免疫制动器之外,SIRT 对精子发生也至关重要。我们的数据表明,SIRT 上调通过自噬和凋亡抑制促进细胞存活,从而有利于病毒复制,同时通过抑制 NF-κB 和干扰素信号传导抑制先天免疫。资金来源 R03AI188484 主题类别 病毒免疫学 (VIR)
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
猴痘病毒 (MPXV) clade IIb 的全球 暴发 包括约 106,000 例病例,主要发生在男性中,引起了对性 传播 的严重关注。痘病毒通过阻断病原体感应和抑制 抗病毒药物 反应来逃避免疫检测。
设计与对象
我们对人类睾丸细胞中的 MPXV 感染进行了表征,以深入了解细胞类型特异性睾丸对感染的反应。人原代支持细胞 (SC) 和间质细胞 (LC) 被 MPXV clade IIb (MOI 0.005) 感染。使用感染后第 1 天、第 3 天和第 5 天 (dpi) 感染的 SC 和 LC 的 RNA 样本进行 RNA 测序分析。
核心方法
通过定量 PCR 和噬菌斑测定证实感染。根据严格的截断值(log2 倍数变化 >|1.5|;调整 p 值 < 0.05)鉴定差异表达基因 (DEG),并使用 Ingenuity Pathway Analysis 分析病毒反应和类固醇生成途径的富集。
主要结果
在 SC 和 LC 中均证实了生产性感染,病毒滴度在 5 dpi 时达到峰值,没有明显的细胞死亡。尽管病毒复制在 1 dpi 时就很明显,但 DEG 的数量仍然很低(LC 中为 86 个,SC 中为 51 个),而两种细胞类型在 5 dpi 时均超过 3000 个。值得注意的是,典型的病毒传感、细胞死亡和炎症途径在任何时间点都没有显示任何一种细胞类型的显着激活。
解释与创新
这是首个证据表明 MPXV 可以感染关键的人类睾丸类型并抑制关键的 抗病毒药物 途径,类似于牛痘病毒。我们的数据表明,SIRT 上调通过自噬和凋亡抑制促进细胞存活,从而有利于病毒复制,同时通过抑制 NF-κB 和干扰素信号传导抑制先天免疫。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,睾丸细胞中的 MPXV 感染可能通过抑制 抗病毒药物 反应来促进病毒持续存在和性 传播。这凸显了在 MPXV 监测 和 治疗 策略中考虑睾丸组织的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究是在体外使用原代人类睾丸细胞进行的,该细胞可能无法在体内条件下完全复制。由于研究范围有限且缺乏体内验证,证据强度中等。

Mpox virus can suppress virus sensing and antiviral Responses in testicular cells: potential role of Sirtuins 2310247

Authors: Matthew Putnam, Johnathan Cornell, Saguna Verma
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction The global outbreak of Mpox virus (MPXV) clade IIb, comprising ∼106,000 cases predominantly in men, has raised serious concerns regarding sexual transmission. Pox viruses evade immune detection by blocking pathogen sensing and inhibiting antiviral responses. We here characterized MPXV infection in human testicular cells to gain insights into the landscape of cell-type-specific testicular response to infection. Methods Human primary Sertoli (SC) and Leydig (LC) cells were infected with MPXV clade IIb (MOI 0.005). Infection was confirmed by quantitative PCR and plaque assays. RNA-seq analysis was conducted using RNA samples from infected SC and LC at days 1, 3, and 5 post-infection (dpi). Differentially expressed genes (DEGs) were identified based on strict cutoffs (log2 fold-change >|1.5|; adj. p-value < 0.05) and analyzed for enrichment in virus-response and steroidogenesis pathways using Ingenuity Pathway Analysis. Results Productive infection was confirmed in both SC and LC with peak virus titers at 5 dpi without significant cell death. Although viral replication was evident by 1 dpi, the number of DEGs remained low (86 in LC and 51 in SC), while they were above 3000 at 5 dpi in both cell types. Notably, canonical viral sensing, cell death, and inflammatory pathways showed no significant activation in either cell type at any time point. Sirtuin family genes (SIRTs) were upregulated at 3 and 5 dpi, correlating with downregulation of antiviral response genes, including DNA virus-sensing gene IFI16. Conclusion This is the first evidence that MPXV can infect key human testicular types and suppress key antiviral pathways similar to vaccinia virus. In addition to functioning as post-virus sensing immune brakes, SIRTs are also crucial for spermatogenesis. Our data suggest that SIRT upregulation favors viral replication by promoting cell survival via autophagy and apoptosis inhibition, while simultaneously dampening innate immunity through suppression of NF-κB and interferon signaling. Funding Source R03AI188484 Topic Categories Viral Immunology (VIR)
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The global outbreak of Mpox virus (MPXV) clade IIb, comprising ∼106,000 cases predominantly in men, has raised serious concerns regarding sexual transmission. Pox viruses evade immune detection by blocking pathogen sensing and inhibiting antiviral responses.
Design and population
We characterized MPXV infection in human testicular cells to gain insights into the landscape of cell-type-specific testicular response to infection. Human primary Sertoli (SC) and Leydig (LC) cells were infected with MPXV clade IIb (MOI 0.005). RNA-seq analysis was conducted using RNA samples from infected SC and LC at days 1, 3, and 5 post-infection (dpi).
Core methods
Infection was confirmed by quantitative PCR and plaque assays. Differentially expressed genes (DEGs) were identified based on strict cutoffs (log2 fold-change >|1.5|; adj. p-value < 0.05) and analyzed for enrichment in virus-response and steroidogenesis pathways using Ingenuity Pathway Analysis.
Main results
Productive infection was confirmed in both SC and LC with peak virus titers at 5 dpi without significant cell death. Although viral replication was evident by 1 dpi, the number of DEGs remained low (86 in LC and 51 in SC), while they were above 3000 at 5 dpi in both cell types. Notably, canonical viral sensing, cell death, and inflammatory pathways showed no significant activation in either cell type at any time point.
Interpretation and novelty
This is the first evidence that MPXV can infect key human testicular types and suppress key antiviral pathways similar to vaccinia virus. Our data suggest that SIRT upregulation favors viral replication by promoting cell survival via autophagy and apoptosis inhibition, while simultaneously dampening innate immunity through suppression of NF-κB and interferon signaling.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that MPXV infection in testicular cells may contribute to viral persistence and sexual transmission by suppressing antiviral responses. This highlights the importance of considering testicular tissue in MPXV surveillance and treatment strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in vitro using primary human testicular cells, which may not fully replicate in vivo conditions. The evidence strength is moderate due to the limited scope of the study and the lack of in vivo validation.

猴痘 有效支持非洲应对工作的研究协调:经验教训、挑战和前进方向

Mpox research coordination to effectively support response efforts in Africa: Lessons learned, challenges, and way forward
作者： Mosoka P. Fallah, Olayinka Stephen Ilesanmi, Abigael Abiy Mesfin, Shahd O.S. Osman, Oluwatoyosi Olawande, Tamrat Shaweno, Yap Boum II, Nebiyu Dereje, Ngashi Ngongo, Jean Kaseya
摘要中文翻译由 猴痘病毒 引起的 猴痘 已从流行地区的孤立病例发展为影响非洲多个国家的大范围 暴发。大陆事件管理支持团队 (IMST) 的研究和创新支柱的建立是为了生成支持响应工作的证据,包括分析整个非洲 猴痘 临床试验和健康研究的效率和效果。本文介绍了 猴痘 研究工作所取得的成就、面临的挑战以及吸取的教训。非洲疾病控制和预防中心 (非洲 CDC) 对 猴痘 应对措施的研究贡献对于管理整个非洲大陆的传染病 暴发 至关重要。各成员国协调了多项研究举措,包括社会行为、流行病学、诊断、疫苗 和治疗研究。主要成就包括建立协调的大陆研究平台、启动多国研究以及绘制整个非洲正在进行的 猴痘 研究活动。挑战包括官僚程序、资金支付延迟、伦理审批瓶颈、协调复杂性以及影响研究及时实施的 监测 差距。解决这些差距有可能加强基于证据的决策,并为改善非洲未来的流行病防范和应对提供宝贵的经验教训。贡献:猴痘 研究展示了协作研究工作的活力、对其进展进行持续、系统监测的必要性,以及实时传播研究结果的必要性,以便为确保有效应对 猴痘 的实践提供信息。从早期社区参与开始,实时传播研究成果对于确保研究成果转化为行动至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究涉及非洲的 进化 或 猴痘 从孤立病例到广泛传播的 暴发,旨在协调研究工作以支持应对举措。
设计与对象
本文介绍了各成员国协调的 猴痘 研究工作所取得的成就、挑战和经验教训,包括社会行为、流行病学、诊断、疫苗 和治疗研究。
核心方法
大陆事件管理支持团队 (IMST) 的研究和创新支柱的建立是为了生成支持响应工作的证据,包括分析整个非洲 猴痘 临床试验和健康研究的效率和效果。
主要结果
主要成就包括建立协调的大陆研究平台、启动多国研究以及绘制整个非洲正在进行的 猴痘 研究活动。
解释与创新
猴痘 研究展示了协作研究工作的活力、对其进展进行持续、系统监测的必要性,以及实时传播研究成果以告知实践的必要性,确保有效应对 猴痘。
科研与公卫意义
弥补研究差距有可能加强基于证据的决策,并为改善非洲未来的流行病防范和应对提供宝贵的经验教训。
局限与证据强度
挑战包括官僚程序、资金支付延迟、伦理审批瓶颈、协调复杂性以及影响研究及时实施的 监测 差距,由于这些限制,证据强度被评估为中等。

Mpox research coordination to effectively support response efforts in Africa: Lessons learned, challenges, and way forward

Authors: Mosoka P. Fallah, Olayinka Stephen Ilesanmi, Abigael Abiy Mesfin, Shahd O.S. Osman, Oluwatoyosi Olawande, Tamrat Shaweno, Yap Boum II, Nebiyu Dereje, Ngashi Ngongo, Jean Kaseya
Original abstract (English)
Mpox, caused by the mpox virus, has evolved from isolated cases in endemic regions to widespread outbreaks affecting multiple countries in Africa. The Research and Innovation pillar of the Continental Incident Management Support Team (IMST) was established to generate evidence that supports response efforts, including analysing the efficiency and effectiveness of clinical trials and health research on mpox across Africa. This article describes the achievements, challenges faced and lessons learned from mpox research efforts. The contributions of research to the mpox response by the Africa Centres for Disease Control and Prevention (Africa CDC) are crucial to managing infectious disease outbreaks across the continent. Multiple research initiatives were coordinated across Member States, including socio-behavioural, epidemiological, diagnostic, vaccine and therapeutic studies. Key achievements included the establishment of a coordinated continental research platform, initiation of multi-country studies and mapping of ongoing mpox research activities across Africa. Challenges included bureaucratic procedures, delays in funding disbursement, ethical approval bottlenecks, coordination complexities and surveillance gaps that affected timely research implementation. Addressing these gaps has the potential to enhance evidence-based decision-making and provide valuable lessons for improving future epidemic preparedness and response in Africa. Contribution: The mpox research demonstrated the vitality of collaborative research efforts, the need for continuous, systematic monitoring of its progress and the need for real-time dissemination of research findings to inform practices that ensure an effective response to mpox. Real-time dissemination of research findings, starting with early community engagement, is critical to ensure that research findings are translated into action.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the evolution of mpox from isolated cases to widespread outbreaks in Africa and aims to coordinate research efforts to support response initiatives.
Design and population
This article describes the achievements, challenges, and lessons learned from mpox research efforts coordinated across Member States, including socio-behavioural, epidemiological, diagnostic, vaccine, and therapeutic studies.
Core methods
The Research and Innovation pillar of the Continental Incident Management Support Team (IMST) was established to generate evidence supporting response efforts, including analyzing the efficiency and effectiveness of clinical trials and health research on mpox across Africa.
Main results
Key achievements included establishing a coordinated continental research platform, initiating multi-country studies, and mapping ongoing mpox research activities across Africa.
Interpretation and novelty
The mpox research demonstrated the vitality of collaborative research efforts, the need for continuous, systematic monitoring of its progress, and the need for real-time dissemination of research findings to inform practices ensuring an effective response to mpox.
Scientific and public-health significance
Addressing research gaps has the potential to enhance evidence-based decision-making and provide valuable lessons for improving future epidemic preparedness and response in Africa.
Limitations and evidence strength
Challenges included bureaucratic procedures, delays in funding disbursement, ethical approval bottlenecks, coordination complexities, and surveillance gaps that affected timely research implementation, with evidence strength assessed as moderate due to these limitations.

双模式重组酶聚合酶扩增平台,通过荧光和生物传感同时检测猴痘病毒并进行进化枝分化

Dual-mode recombinase polymerase amplification platform for simultaneous detection and clade differentiation of monkeypox virus via fluorescence and biosensing
作者： Juan Zhou, Linglong Wan, Yifei Xu, Min Chen, Xiaolan Huang, Fei Xiao, Jin Fu, Nan Jia, Yu Zhang, Luxi Jiang
摘要中文翻译猴痘病毒(MPXV)继续对全球公共卫生构成重大挑战,凸显了快速分子诊断的迫切必要性,该诊断能够同时识别病毒感染并区分流行的进化枝。在本研究中,我们开发了一种基于荧光和侧流生物传感器 (LFB) 的双链重组酶聚合酶扩增 (RPA) 平台,用于同时检测进化枝 I 和进化枝 II MPXV。通过将 RPA 技术与基于荧光的实时监测或基于 LFB 的视觉读数相结合,双目标放大过程可转化为灵活且信息丰富的检测输出。在单靶标条件下,检测结果一致地检测到分支 I 和分支 II MPXV,浓度分别低至 6.8 × 10 -1 和 7.7 × 10 -1 拷贝/μL。尽管在双工条件下灵敏度下降,但两个目标在单个反应中仍然可以可靠地检测到。该测定进一步证明了高分析特异性,与非 MPXV 病原体未表现出可检测到的交叉反应性。该平台在模拟临床样本(包括血清、皮肤拭子和鼻咽拭子)中表现出一致的定性性能,同时表明基质对检测灵敏度的潜在影响。从扩增到结果解释的整个检测工作流程可以在 25 分钟内完成。提供两种可选的读数格式有助于适应集中实验室分析和分散或即时检测场景。总体而言,该双工 RPA 平台为 MPXV 监测 和差分 诊断 提供快速、灵敏且多功能的诊断策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
猴痘病毒(MPXV)继续对全球公共卫生构成重大挑战,凸显了快速分子诊断的迫切必要性,该诊断能够同时识别病毒感染并区分流行的进化枝。
设计与对象
在本研究中,我们开发了一种基于荧光和侧流生物传感器 (LFB) 的双链重组酶聚合酶扩增 (RPA) 平台,用于同时检测进化枝 I 和进化枝 II MPXV。
核心方法
通过将 RPA 技术与基于荧光的实时监测或基于 LFB 的视觉读数相结合,双目标放大过程可转化为灵活且信息丰富的检测输出。
主要结果
在单靶标条件下,检测结果一致地检测到分支 I 和分支 II MPXV,浓度分别低至 6.8 × 10 -1 和 7.7 × 10 -1 拷贝/μL。
解释与创新
尽管在双工条件下灵敏度下降,但两个目标在单个反应中仍然可以可靠地检测到。
科研与公卫意义
该测定进一步证明了高分析特异性,与非 MPXV 病原体未表现出可检测到的交叉反应性。
局限与证据强度
该平台在模拟临床样本(包括血清、皮肤拭子和鼻咽拭子)中表现出一致的定性性能,同时表明基质对检测灵敏度的潜在影响。

Dual-mode recombinase polymerase amplification platform for simultaneous detection and clade differentiation of monkeypox virus via fluorescence and biosensing

Authors: Juan Zhou, Linglong Wan, Yifei Xu, Min Chen, Xiaolan Huang, Fei Xiao, Jin Fu, Nan Jia, Yu Zhang, Luxi Jiang
Original abstract (English)
Monkeypox virus (MPXV) continues to present a significant global public health challenge, highlighting the critical necessity for rapid molecular diagnostics capable of simultaneously identifying viral infection and discriminating between circulating clades. In this study, we developed a fluorescent and lateral flow biosensor (LFB)-based duplex recombinase polymerase amplification (RPA) platform for the concurrent detection of clade I and clade II MPXV. By integrating the RPA technique with either fluorescence-based real-time monitoring or LFB-based visual readout, the dual-target amplification process is translated into flexible and informative detection outputs. Under single-target conditions, the assays consistently detected clade I and clade II MPXV at concentrations as low as 6.8 × 10 −1 and 7.7 × 10 −1 copies/μL, respectively. Although sensitivity decreased under duplex conditions, both targets remained reliably detectable within a single reaction. The assays further demonstrated high analytical specificity, exhibiting no detectable cross-reactivity with non-MPXV pathogens. The platform demonstrated consistent qualitative performance in simulated clinical samples, including serum, skin swabs, and nasopharyngeal swabs, while indicating potential matrix effects on detection sensitivity. The entire detection workflow, spanning from amplification to result interpretation, can be completed within 25 min. The provision of two selectable readout formats facilitates adaptation to both centralized laboratory analyses and decentralized or point-of-care testing scenarios. Overall, this duplex RPA platform provides a rapid, sensitive, and versatile diagnostic strategy for MPXV surveillance and differential diagnosis.

对居住在布拉柴维尔的刚果居民有关 猴痘 的知识、看法和预防措施进行评估

Assessment of knowledge, perceptions, and preventive practices regarding Mpox among the Congolese population living in Brazzaville
作者： Dého Yvanhe Mbouala Kianguebeni, Ousmane Souare, Claujens Chastel Mfoutou Mapanguy, Jeannhey Christevy Vouvoungui, Oluwatoyosi Olawande, Nafissatou Diallo, Jethro Peya Massala, Marliti Ngambou Nguissaliki, Carine Kades, Olayinka Ilesanmi Stephen
摘要中文翻译猴痘(以前称为猴痘)重新成为全球健康威胁。然而,人们对非洲城市人口的公众意识和反应知之甚少。刚果共和国几十年来经历了零星的 猴痘 暴发,首都布拉柴维尔被确定为 2025 年 暴发 的震中。尽管如此,还没有研究评估人们对 猴痘 的知识、态度和实践 (KAP)。本研究旨在评估刚果居民对 猴痘 的 KAP 程度。2025 年 3 月,布拉柴维尔针对出现 猴痘 确诊病例的卫生区开展了一项基于社区的横断面调查。采用三阶段整群抽样方法从不同社会背景中选择 15 岁及以上的个体。通过标准化 KAP 问卷收集数据,并使用多变量逻辑回归进行分析,以确定与知识、看法和实践相关的因素。共有 567 名参与者参加(46% 男性,54% 女性)。总体而言,89.6% (95% CI 86.8–91.8) 的受访者对 猴痘 的了解较差,而 10.4% (95% CI 8.2–13.2) 的受访者获得了良好的知识得分。只有 7.4% (95% CI 5.5–9.9) 正确识别病毒为病因,38.0% (95% CI 34.1–42.0) 以前听说过这种疾病。61.0% (95% CI 57.1–65.1) 的参与者认为存在中度至低风险,67.0% (95% CI 63.0–70.8) 的参与者表现出不良的预防措施。更好的知识与中等教育(aOR = 2.48;95% CI 1.38–4.45)、中等收入(aOR = 0.13;95% CI 0.05–0.35)和女性(aOR = 0.35;95% CI 0.18–0.64)相关。吉里和塔兰盖地区的政府雇员和居民报告了更好的做法。只有 28% 的人对假设的 猴痘 疫苗 表示强烈信任,19% 的人认为大多数社区成员会接受 疫苗接种。尽管 暴发 正在进行,但布拉柴维尔与 猴痘 相关的知识、认知和预防行为仍然低得惊人。迫切需要扩大公众意识活动并加强沟通战略,以建立信任并加强刚果共和国的 暴发 准备和响应。

问题与背景
猴痘(以前称为猴痘)重新成为全球健康威胁。然而,人们对非洲城市人口的公众意识和反应知之甚少。
设计与对象
2025 年 3 月,布拉柴维尔针对出现 猴痘 确诊病例的卫生区开展了一项基于社区的横断面调查。共有 567 名参与者参加(46% 男性,54% 女性)。
核心方法
通过标准化 KAP 问卷收集数据,并使用多变量逻辑回归进行分析,以确定与知识、看法和实践相关的因素。
主要结果
总体而言,89.6% (95% CI 86.8–91.8) 的受访者对 猴痘 的了解较差,而 10.4% (95% CI 8.2–13.2) 的受访者获得了良好的知识得分。只有 7.4% (95% CI 5.5–9.9) 正确识别病毒为病因,38.0% (95% CI 34.1–42.0) 以前听说过这种疾病。61.0% (95% CI 57.1–65.1) 的参与者认为存在中度至低风险,67.0% (95% CI 63.0–70.8) 的参与者表现出不良的预防措施。
解释与创新
更好的知识与中等教育(aOR = 2.48;95% CI 1.38–4.45)、中等收入(aOR = 0.13;95% CI 0.05–0.35)和女性(aOR = 0.35;95% CI 0.18–0.64)相关。吉里和塔兰盖地区的政府雇员和居民报告了更好的做法。
科研与公卫意义
只有 28% 的人对假设的 猴痘 疫苗 表示强烈信任,19% 的人认为大多数社区成员会接受 疫苗接种。迫切需要扩大公众意识活动并加强沟通战略,以建立信任并加强刚果共和国的 暴发 准备和响应。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计或证据强度评估的局限性。

Assessment of knowledge, perceptions, and preventive practices regarding Mpox among the Congolese population living in Brazzaville

Authors: Dého Yvanhe Mbouala Kianguebeni, Ousmane Souare, Claujens Chastel Mfoutou Mapanguy, Jeannhey Christevy Vouvoungui, Oluwatoyosi Olawande, Nafissatou Diallo, Jethro Peya Massala, Marliti Ngambou Nguissaliki, Carine Kades, Olayinka Ilesanmi Stephen
Original abstract (English)
Mpox (formerly known as monkeypox) has re-emerged as a global health threat. However, little is known about public awareness and responses in urban African populations. The Republic of Congo has experienced sporadic Mpox outbreaks over several decades, with Brazzaville, the capital, identified as the epicenter of the 2025 outbreak. Despite this, no study has assessed the knowledge, attitudes, and practices (KAP) of the population regarding Mpox. This study aimed to evaluate the KAP of Congolese residents toward Mpox. A cross-sectional, community-based survey was conducted in March 2025 in Brazzaville, targeting health districts with confirmed Mpox cases. A three-stage cluster sampling method was used to select individuals aged 15 and older from diverse social backgrounds. Data were collected through a standardized KAP questionnaire and analyzed using multivariable logistic regression to identify factors associated with knowledge, perceptions, and practices. A total of 567 participants were enrolled (46% male, 54% female). Overall, 89.6% (95% CI 86.8–91.8) demonstrated poor knowledge of Mpox, while 10.4% (95% CI 8.2–13.2) achieved a good knowledge score. Only 7.4% (95% CI 5.5–9.9) correctly identified a virus as the cause, and 38.0% (95% CI 34.1–42.0) had previously heard of the disease. Moderate to low-risk perception was observed in 61.0% (95% CI 57.1–65.1), and 67.0% (95% CI 63.0–70.8) of participants exhibited poor preventive practices. Better knowledge was associated with secondary education (aOR = 2.48; 95% CI 1.38–4.45), middle income (aOR = 0.13; 95% CI 0.05–0.35), and female gender (aOR = 0.35; 95% CI 0.18–0.64). Government employees and residents of Djiri and Talangaï districts reported better practices. Only 28% expressed strong trust in a hypothetical Mpox vaccine, and 19% believed that most community members would accept vaccination. Despite the ongoing outbreak, knowledge, perception, and preventive behaviors related to Mpox remain alarmingly low in Brazzaville. There is an urgent need for expanded public awareness campaigns and enhanced communication strategies to build trust and strengthen outbreak preparedness and response in the Republic of Congo.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Mpox (formerly known as monkeypox) has re-emerged as a global health threat. However, little is known about public awareness and responses in urban African populations.
Design and population
A cross-sectional, community-based survey was conducted in March 2025 in Brazzaville, targeting health districts with confirmed Mpox cases. A total of 567 participants were enrolled (46% male, 54% female).
Core methods

Main results
Overall, 89.6% (95% CI 86.8–91.8) demonstrated poor knowledge of Mpox, while 10.4% (95% CI 8.2–13.2) achieved a good knowledge score. Only 7.4% (95% CI 5.5–9.9) correctly identified a virus as the cause, and 38.0% (95% CI 34.1–42.0) had previously heard of the disease. Moderate to low-risk perception was observed in 61.0% (95% CI 57.1–65.1), and 67.0% (95% CI 63.0–70.8) of participants exhibited poor preventive practices.

Better knowledge was associated with secondary education (aOR = 2.48; 95% CI 1.38–4.45), middle income (aOR = 0.13; 95% CI 0.05–0.35), and female gender (aOR = 0.35; 95% CI 0.18–0.64). Government employees and residents of Djiri and Talangaï districts reported better practices.

Only 28% expressed strong trust in a hypothetical Mpox vaccine, and 19% believed that most community members would accept vaccination. There is an urgent need for expanded public awareness campaigns and enhanced communication strategies to build trust and strengthen outbreak preparedness and response in the Republic of Congo.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations of the study design or evidence strength assessment.

治疗 的 猴痘 和 特考韦瑞 在中非共和国扩大访问使用。

Treatment of mpox with tecovirimat under expanded access use in the Central African Republic.
作者： Josephine Bourner, Festus Mbrenga, Elise Pesonel, Aboubacar Soumah, Sandra Garba-Ouangole, Ella Farra, Cyprien Lemon, Christian Malaka, Jake Dunning, Amanda Rojek
摘要中文翻译目标:该计划的主要目的是为疑似 猴痘 的患者提供 特考韦瑞 的使用权。次要目标是描述 Clade Ia 猴痘 的临床和病毒学特征并评估 特考韦瑞 的安全性。该计划还支持加强研究能力、通过 监测 参与改进病例发现以及标准化病例管理。方法:姆拜基地区医院和班吉总医院实施了扩大就诊计划。同意疑似 猴痘 的患者按照标准化方案接受口服 特考韦瑞 14 天。随访发生在第 21 天(如果第 14 天 PCR 呈阳性)和第 28 天或更晚。每次访视时收集血液和病变样本。结果:31 名患者入组;26 例 (84%) 为 PCR 阳性,24 例 (77%) 为 Ia 型 猴痘。症状多种多样,最常见的是淋巴结肿大、头痛、肌痛和活动性病变。大多数症状在第 14 天和最后一次就诊时有所改善,但淋巴结肿大常常持续存在。PCR 阳性率随着时间的推移而下降,但在某些情况下仍然可以检测到。五名患者发生六次严重不良事件。结论:虽然没有证明 治疗 具有明显的益处,但该计划提高了对疾病的了解并增强了临床和研究能力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在为疑似 特考韦瑞 的患者提供 特考韦瑞 的使用机会,并描述 Clade Ia 猴痘 的临床和病毒学特征,同时评估 特考韦瑞 的安全性。
设计与对象
姆拜基区医院和班吉综合医院实施了扩大准入计划,招募了同意的疑似 猴痘 患者。
核心方法
患者按照标准化方案接受口服 特考韦瑞 14 天,并在第 21 天(如果第 14 天 PCR 呈阳性)和第 28 天或之后进行随访,在此期间收集血液和病变样本。
主要结果
共有 31 名患者入组;26 例 (84%) 为 PCR 阳性,24 例 (77%) 为 Ia 型 猴痘。症状多种多样,最常见的是淋巴结肿大、头痛、肌痛和活动性病变,大多数在第 14 天和最后一次就诊时有所改善,但淋巴结肿大常常持续存在。五名患者发生六次严重不良事件。
解释与创新
虽然没有证明 治疗 具有明显的益处,但该计划提高了对疾病的了解并增强了临床和研究能力。
科研与公卫意义
该计划支持加强研究能力,通过 监测 参与改进病例检测,并标准化 猴痘 的病例管理。
局限与证据强度
尽管该研究提供了有关 特考韦瑞 在资源有限的环境中使用的有价值的真实数据,但由于样本量较小且缺乏对照组,证据强度中等。

Treatment of mpox with tecovirimat under expanded access use in the Central African Republic.

Authors: Josephine Bourner, Festus Mbrenga, Elise Pesonel, Aboubacar Soumah, Sandra Garba-Ouangole, Ella Farra, Cyprien Lemon, Christian Malaka, Jake Dunning, Amanda Rojek
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVES: This programme primarily aimed to provide access to tecovirimat for patients with suspected mpox. Secondary objectives were to describe the clinical and virological features of Clade Ia mpox and assess the safety of tecovirimat. The programme also supported research capacity strengthening, improved case detection through surveillance engagement, and standardised case management. METHODS: An expanded access programme was implemented at Mbaiki District Hospital and Bangui General Hospital. Consenting patients with suspected mpox received oral tecovirimat for 14 days under a standardised protocol. Follow-up visits occurred at day 21 (if PCR-positive at day 14) and day 28 or later. Blood and lesion samples were collected at each visit. RESULTS: Thirty-one patients were enrolled; 26 (84%) were PCR-positive and 24 (77%) had Clade Ia mpox. Symptoms were heterogeneous, most commonly lymphadenopathy, headache, myalgia, and active lesions. Most symptoms improved by day 14 and the final visit, though lymphadenopathy often persisted. PCR positivity declined over time but remained detectable in some cases. Six serious adverse events occurred in five patients. CONCLUSIONS: While no clear treatment benefit was demonstrated, the programme improved understanding of disease and strengthened clinical and research capacity.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to provide access to tecovirimat for patients with suspected mpox and to describe the clinical and virological features of Clade Ia mpox while assessing the safety of tecovirimat.
Design and population
An expanded access programme was implemented at Mbaiki District Hospital and Bangui General Hospital, enrolling consenting patients with suspected mpox.
Core methods
Patients received oral tecovirimat for 14 days under a standardised protocol, with follow-up visits at day 21 (if PCR-positive at day 14) and day 28 or later, during which blood and lesion samples were collected.
Main results
Thirty-one patients were enrolled; 26 (84%) were PCR-positive and 24 (77%) had Clade Ia mpox. Symptoms were heterogeneous, most commonly lymphadenopathy, headache, myalgia, and active lesions, with most improving by day 14 and the final visit, though lymphadenopathy often persisted. Six serious adverse events occurred in five patients.
Interpretation and novelty
While no clear treatment benefit was demonstrated, the programme improved understanding of disease and strengthened clinical and research capacity.
Scientific and public-health significance
The programme supported research capacity strengthening, improved case detection through surveillance engagement, and standardised case management for mpox.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the small sample size and lack of a control group, though the study provides valuable real-world data on tecovirimat use in a resource-limited setting.
