具有可解释变压器模型的自动多标签 猴痘 研究分类

Automated Multilabel Mpox Research Classification with Explainable Transformer Models
作者： Tanjim Taharat Aurpa
摘要中文翻译猴痘 暴发 仍然是一个严重的公共卫生问题,世界卫生组织(WHO)报告称,一些地区的病例不断增加。对 猴痘 的研究至关重要,原因有几个,包括 疫苗 开发、诊断改进、病毒 进化 研究以及预防未来的 暴发。然而,大量发表的研究使得有效组织和分析信息变得困难。本研究重点使用多标签分类将 14590 篇 猴痘 研究文章分类为关键主题,例如 暴发、疫苗接种 和 流行病学。在测试的不同AI模型中,BERT表现最好,准确率达到97.05%,微观F1得分为97.67%,宏观F1得分为96.46%。为了更好地理解模型如何做出决策,使用 SHAP 来分析重要的单词特征和模式。结果表明,BERT 可以帮助对 猴痘 研究进行自动化分类,使研究人员、政策制定者和医疗保健工作者更容易快速找到相关信息,从而节省时间并改善公共卫生工作。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
猴痘 暴发 仍然是一个严重的公共卫生问题,世界卫生组织报告一些地区的病例不断增加,对 猴痘 的研究对于 疫苗 的开发、诊断改进、病毒 进化 研究以及预防未来的 暴发 至关重要。
设计与对象
本研究重点使用多标签分类将 14,590 篇 猴痘 研究文章分类为关键主题,例如 暴发、疫苗接种 和 流行病学。
核心方法
在测试的不同AI模型中,BERT表现最好,准确率达到97.05%,微观F1得分为97.67%,宏观F1得分为96.46%,SHAP用于分析重要的单词特征和模式。
主要结果
BERT 将 猴痘 研究文章分类为关键主题时,准确率达到 97.05%,微观 F1 得分为 97.67%,宏观 F1 得分为 96.46%。
解释与创新
结果表明,BERT 可以帮助对 猴痘 研究进行自动化分类,使研究人员、政策制定者和医疗工作者更容易快速找到相关信息。
科研与公卫意义
自动分类系统可有效组织和分析 猴痘 研究,从而节省时间并改善公共卫生工作。
局限与证据强度
该研究展示了 BERT 对 猴痘 研究文章的多标签分类的高性能,根据报告的准确性和 F1 分数评估证据强度为高。

Automated Multilabel Mpox Research Classification with Explainable Transformer Models

Authors: Tanjim Taharat Aurpa
Original abstract (English)
The Mpox outbreak remains a serious public health issue, with the WHO (World Health Organization) reporting increasing cases in some regions. Research on Mpox is vital for several reasons, including vaccine development, diagnostic improvement, viral evolution studies, and preventing future outbreaks. However, the large amount of research being published makes it difficult to organize and analyze information efficiently. This study focuses on using multilabel classification to categorize 14590 Mpox research articles into key topics such as outbreaks, vaccination, and epidemiology. Among the different AI models tested, BERT performed the best, achieving 97.05% accuracy, 97.67% micro F1 score, and 96.46% macro F1 score. To better understand how the model makes decisions, SHAP was used to analyze significant word features and patterns. The results show that BERT can help automate the classification of Mpox research, making it easier for researchers, policymakers, and healthcare workers to quickly find relevant information, saving time and improving public health efforts.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The Mpox outbreak remains a serious public health issue, with the WHO reporting increasing cases in some regions, and research on Mpox is vital for vaccine development, diagnostic improvement, viral evolution studies, and preventing future outbreaks.
Design and population
This study focuses on using multilabel classification to categorize 14,590 Mpox research articles into key topics such as outbreaks, vaccination, and epidemiology.
Core methods
Among the different AI models tested, BERT performed the best, achieving 97.05% accuracy, 97.67% micro F1 score, and 96.46% macro F1 score, and SHAP was used to analyze significant word features and patterns.
Main results
BERT achieved 97.05% accuracy, 97.67% micro F1 score, and 96.46% macro F1 score in classifying Mpox research articles into key topics.
Interpretation and novelty
The results show that BERT can help automate the classification of Mpox research, making it easier for researchers, policymakers, and healthcare workers to quickly find relevant information.
Scientific and public-health significance
The automated classification system saves time and improves public health efforts by enabling efficient organization and analysis of Mpox research.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study demonstrates high performance of BERT for multilabel classification of Mpox research articles, with evidence strength assessed as high based on the reported accuracy and F1 scores.

合作应对输入性传染病的成功跨机构模式:中国上海首例确诊的 Ia 分支 猴痘 病例的调查和管理

A Successful Cross-Agency Model for Collaborative Response to Imported Infectious Disease: Investigation and Management of the First Confirmed Clade Ia Mpox Case in Shanghai, China
作者： Zilian Cao, Fang Xu, Xiaoyan Huang, Yifeng Shen, Xin Xin, Lin Ai, Hao Pan, Huanyu Wu, Hào Pān
摘要中文翻译简介:从历史上看,人类感染 Ia 分支猴痘病毒 (MPXV) 主要通过人畜共患 传播 发生,并有有限的继发性人传人 传播。最近的研究表明,越来越多的证据表明某些 Ia 分支谱系存在人传人 传播 的情况。本报告记录了上海首例输入型 Ia 猴痘 病例,也是中国记录的第二例病例。方法:通过对患者和主治医生进行访谈,获得患者人口统计资料、临床概况、流行病学史和密切接触者。收集临床标本进行实时荧光定量聚合酶链反应(qPCR)和全 基因组 测序。实施接触者追踪,对密切接触者进行21天的健康监测。结果:患者出现中度症状,包括发烧、皮疹和呼吸道症状。基因组 测序鉴定该病毒为进化枝 Ia,与来自刚果民主共和国 (DRC) 的病毒株显示出 99.96% 的核苷酸同一性。确定了 8 名密切接触者,没有报告继发感染。结论:该病例被确认为源自刚果民主共和国的输入性分支Ia 猴痘病例。疾控中心与海关“四联”跨部门协作机制,为有效遏制当地传播发挥了重要作用。远程调查的挑战凸显了加强国际合作的必要性。建立“预警-控制-追踪”一体化体系,配合公共卫生干预,对于降低输入风险、维护国家卫生安全至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这项研究需要记录和管理中国上海首例输入性 Ia 分支 猴痘 病例,因为最近的研究表明越来越多的证据表明某些 Ia 分支谱系存在人传人的 传播 病例。
设计与对象
这是对中国上海一名输入型 Ia 猴痘 患者的病例调查,涉及患者访谈、临床标本采集和接触者追踪。
核心方法
通过访谈获得患者人口统计资料、临床概况、流行病学史和密切接触者;使用 qPCR 和全 基因组 测序分析临床标本;实施了接触者追踪和 21 天健康监测。
主要结果
患者出现中度症状,包括发烧、皮疹和呼吸道症状;基因组 测序鉴定该病毒为 Ia 分支,与 DRC 毒株具有 99.96% 的核苷酸同一性;确定了 8 名密切接触者,没有报告继发感染。
解释与创新
作者将此案例解读为成功展示了“四个联合”跨机构协作机制在阻止本地传播方面的有效性,凸显了这种综合方法应对输入性传染病的新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了建立具有公共卫生干预措施的综合“预警-控制-跟踪”系统的重要性,以降低输入风险并维护国家卫生安全。
局限与证据强度
人们注意到远程调查面临的挑战,但证据强度很高,因为这是一个有据可查的输入病例,具有经过证实的实验室结果和成功的遏制。

A Successful Cross-Agency Model for Collaborative Response to Imported Infectious Disease: Investigation and Management of the First Confirmed Clade Ia Mpox Case in Shanghai, China

Authors: Zilian Cao, Fang Xu, Xiaoyan Huang, Yifeng Shen, Xin Xin, Lin Ai, Hao Pan, Huanyu Wu, Hào Pān
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: Historically, human infections with clade Ia monkeypox virus (MPXV) primarily occurred through zoonotic transmission with limited secondary human-to-human transmission. Recent studies suggest increasing evidence of human-to-human transmission of some clade Ia lineages. This report documents the first imported clade Ia mpox case in Shanghai, representing the second documented case in China. Methods: Patient demographics, clinical profiles, epidemiological history, and close contacts were obtained through interviews with the patient and attending physicians. Clinical specimens were collected for real-time fluorescent quantitative polymerase chain reaction (qPCR) and whole-genome sequencing. Contact tracing was implemented, involving a 21-day health monitoring of close contacts. Results: The patient presented with moderate symptoms, including fever, rash, and respiratory manifestations. Genome sequencing identified the virus as clade Ia, showing 99.96% nucleotide identity to a strain from the Democratic Republic of Congo (DRC). Eight close contacts were identified with no secondary infection reported. Conclusions: This case was confirmed as an imported clade Ia mpox case originating in the DRC. The “four JOINTs” cross-agency collaborative mechanism between the CDC and customs played a significant role in effectively stopping local transmission. Challenges in remote investigation highlight the need for stronger international collaboration. Establishing an integrated “early warning-control-tracking” system, coupled with public health intervention, is crucial for reducing importation risks and safeguarding national health security.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study was needed to document and manage the first imported clade Ia mpox case in Shanghai, China, as recent studies suggest increasing evidence of human-to-human transmission of some clade Ia lineages.
Design and population
This was a case investigation of an imported clade Ia mpox patient in Shanghai, China, involving patient interviews, clinical specimen collection, and contact tracing.
Core methods
Patient demographics, clinical profiles, epidemiological history, and close contacts were obtained through interviews; clinical specimens were analyzed using qPCR and whole-genome sequencing; contact tracing with 21-day health monitoring was implemented.
Main results
The patient presented with moderate symptoms including fever, rash, and respiratory manifestations; genome sequencing identified the virus as clade Ia with 99.96% nucleotide identity to a DRC strain; eight close contacts were identified with no secondary infections reported.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted this case as successfully demonstrating the effectiveness of the 'four JOINTs' cross-agency collaborative mechanism in stopping local transmission, highlighting the novelty of this integrated approach for imported infectious diseases.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings emphasize the importance of establishing an integrated 'early warning-control-tracking' system with public health interventions to reduce importation risks and safeguard national health security.
Limitations and evidence strength
Challenges in remote investigation were noted, but the evidence strength is high as this represents a well-documented imported case with confirmed laboratory results and successful containment.

猴痘 中国重庆和成都MSM知识水平及相关因素

Mpox knowledge levels and associated factors among MSM in Chongqing and Chengdu, China
作者： Liping Fei, Jing Lin, Chao Zhou, Wei Zhang, Jingpei Xu, Li Yan, Rui Zhang, Hua Zhao, Wei Huang, Rongrong Lu
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
猴痘(猴痘)已成为全球主要的公共卫生威胁,男男性行为者(MSM)是主要受影响人群。该研究旨在评估中国重庆和成都 MSM 的 猴痘 知识水平和相关因素。
设计与对象
2024 年 8 月至 12 月,通过社区组织便利抽样,对重庆和成都的 MSM 进行了一项横断面研究。共回收有效问卷514份。
核心方法
使用多变量逻辑回归来识别与 猴痘 知识意识相关的因素。采用自行设计的调查问卷收集社会人口特征、性行为、HIV相关状况、猴痘信息关注度和知识水平等数据。
主要结果
猴痘知识总体知晓率为49.6%,知识中位数得分为9.0(IQR:6.0、12.0)。参与者对预防措施(82.1%)和性 传播 途径(80.0%)的反应表现出较高的准确性,而潜伏期(53.5%)和普遍易感性(56.6%)的正确反应较低。
解释与创新
Multivariable analysis showed that having a college education or higher (aOR = 2.47, 95% CI 1.32–4.60), identifying as homosexual (aOR = 1.57, 95% CI 1.01–2.44), absence of group sex in the past year (aOR = 2.55, 95% CI 1.09–5.93), travel to mpox-endemic countries in the past year (aOR = 4.18, 95% CI 1.32–4.60) and actively seeking mpox-related information (aOR = 3.40, 95% CI 2.03–5.71) were independent factors associated with higher mpox knowledge levels.
科研与公卫意义
重庆和成都MSM的猴痘知识知晓水平处于中等水平,知识分布不均匀。猴痘信息的主要来源是互联网和社交媒体(49.8%)。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计或证据强度评估的具体局限性。

Mpox knowledge levels and associated factors among MSM in Chongqing and Chengdu, China

Authors: Liping Fei, Jing Lin, Chao Zhou, Wei Zhang, Jingpei Xu, Li Yan, Rui Zhang, Hua Zhao, Wei Huang, Rongrong Lu
Original abstract (English)
Monkeypox (Mpox) has become a major public health threat globally, with men who have sex with men (MSM) being the primary affected population. In China, the mpox epidemic has spread to multiple provinces, but regional research on MSM in the Chongqing-Chengdu urban agglomeration is lacking. A cross-sectional study was conducted among MSM in Chongqing and Chengdu from August to December 2024 using convenience sampling via community-based organizations. A self-designed questionnaire was used to collect data on socio-demographic characteristics, sexual behaviors, HIV-related status, mpox information attention and knowledge level. Multivariable logistic regression was used to identify factors associated with mpox knowledge awareness. A total of 514 valid questionnaires were collected. The overall awareness rate of mpox knowledge was 49.6%, with a median knowledge score of 9.0 (IQR: 6.0, 12.0). Participants demonstrated high accuracy in responses related to preventive measures (82.1%) and sexual transmission routes (80.0%), whereas correct responses were lower for the incubation period (53.5%) and universal susceptibility (56.6%). The primary source of mpox information was the internet and social media (49.8%). Multivariable analysis showed that having a college education or higher (aOR = 2.47, 95% CI 1.32–4.60), identifying as homosexual (aOR = 1.57, 95% CI 1.01–2.44), absence of group sex in the past year (aOR = 2.55, 95% CI 1.09–5.93), travel to mpox-endemic countries in the past year (aOR = 4.18, 95% CI 1.32–4.60) and actively seeking mpox-related information (aOR = 3.40, 95% CI 2.03–5.71) were independent factors associated with higher mpox knowledge levels. The level of mpox knowledge awareness among MSM in Chongqing and Chengdu was moderate, characterized by an uneven distribution of knowledge. Information acquisition was predominantly through digital channels with insufficient professional input. Targeted health education strategies should be developed for key populations, and an integrated promotion model of “professional personnel + authoritative new media” should be constructed to improve the effectiveness of mpox prevention and control in this population.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Monkeypox (Mpox) has become a major public health threat globally, with men who have sex with men (MSM) being the primary affected population. The study aimed to assess Mpox knowledge levels and associated factors among MSM in Chongqing and Chengdu, China.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted among MSM in Chongqing and Chengdu from August to December 2024 using convenience sampling via community-based organizations. A total of 514 valid questionnaires were collected.
Core methods
Multivariable logistic regression was used to identify factors associated with mpox knowledge awareness. A self-designed questionnaire was used to collect data on socio-demographic characteristics, sexual behaviors, HIV-related status, mpox information attention and knowledge level.
Main results
The overall awareness rate of mpox knowledge was 49.6%, with a median knowledge score of 9.0 (IQR: 6.0, 12.0). Participants demonstrated high accuracy in responses related to preventive measures (82.1%) and sexual transmission routes (80.0%), whereas correct responses were lower for the incubation period (53.5%) and universal susceptibility (56.6%).
Interpretation and novelty
Multivariable analysis showed that having a college education or higher (aOR = 2.47, 95% CI 1.32–4.60), identifying as homosexual (aOR = 1.57, 95% CI 1.01–2.44), absence of group sex in the past year (aOR = 2.55, 95% CI 1.09–5.93), travel to mpox-endemic countries in the past year (aOR = 4.18, 95% CI 1.32–4.60) and actively seeking mpox-related information (aOR = 3.40, 95% CI 2.03–5.71) were independent factors associated with higher mpox knowledge levels.
Scientific and public-health significance
The level of mpox knowledge awareness among MSM in Chongqing and Chengdu was moderate, characterized by an uneven distribution of knowledge. The primary source of mpox information was the internet and social media (49.8%).
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study design or evidence strength assessment.

基于分形分数式Caputo-Katugampola导数的新交叉猴痘病数学模型:数值解

New Crossover Monkeypox Disease Mathematical Model Based on Fractal Fractional Caputo-Katugampola Derivative: Numerical Solutions
作者： Seham AL-Mekhlafi, Ahmed Boudaoui, Noura Laksaci, Thabet Abdeljawad, Hisham M. Alkhawar
摘要中文翻译本文介绍了一种新的猴痘 传播 动力学交叉流行病学模型,将分形-分形 Caputo-Katugampola 导数与分数布朗运动驱动的随机扰动相结合。所提出的框架捕获了两个时间上不同的状态内的复杂记忆、遗传和环境影响:确定性分形-分数阶段和随机分数阶阶段。我们使用广义巴拿赫空间内的佩罗夫不动点定理建立解的存在性和唯一性。为了对系统进行数值求解,我们构建了 $\kappa$-非标准有限差分格式,并采用了适应分数随机设置的修正 Euler-Maruyama 方法。敏感性和分叉分析表明,传播 相关参数是疾病动态的主要驱动因素,导致出现前向分叉,标志着从无病状态到地方病状态的转变。用来自美国的真实 暴发 数据进行校准的数值模拟显示出极好的一致性,并强调了记忆和随机性在塑造感染轨迹中的关键作用。结果强调了分形-分数随机模型在提高预测能力和为新兴流行病中的有针对性的干预策略提供信息方面的实用性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
所提出的框架捕获了两个时间上不同的状态内的复杂记忆、遗传和环境影响:确定性分形-分数阶段和随机分数阶阶段。
设计与对象
提供的证据没有明确说明研究设计、设置或研究人群。
核心方法
本文介绍了一种新的猴痘 传播 动力学交叉流行病学模型,将分形-分形 Caputo-Katugampola 导数与分数布朗运动驱动的随机扰动相结合。
主要结果
提供的证据没有明确说明直接观察到的主要结果。
解释与创新
用来自美国的真实 暴发 数据进行校准的数值模拟显示出极好的一致性,并强调了记忆和随机性在塑造感染轨迹中的关键作用。
科研与公卫意义
结果强调了分形-分数随机模型在提高预测能力和为新兴流行病中的有针对性的干预策略提供信息方面的实用性。
局限与证据强度

New Crossover Monkeypox Disease Mathematical Model Based on Fractal Fractional Caputo-Katugampola Derivative: Numerical Solutions

Authors: Seham AL-Mekhlafi, Ahmed Boudaoui, Noura Laksaci, Thabet Abdeljawad, Hisham M. Alkhawar
Original abstract (English)
This paper introduces a novel crossover epidemiological model for the transmission dynamics of monkeypox, integrating fractal-fractional Caputo-Katugampola derivatives with stochastic perturbations driven by fractional Brownian motion. The proposed framework captures complex memory, hereditary, and environmental effects within two temporally distinct regimes: a deterministic fractal-fractional phase and a stochastic fractional-order phase. We establish the existence and uniqueness of solutions using the Perov fixed-point theorem within generalized Banach spaces. To numerically solve the system, we construct a $\kappa$-nonstandard finite difference scheme and adapted modified Euler-Maruyama method adapted to the fractional stochastic setting.Sensitivity and bifurcation analyses reveal that transmission-related parameters are the primary drivers of disease dynamics, leading to the emergence of a forward bifurcation that marks the transition from disease-free to endemic states. Numerical simulations, calibrated with real outbreak data from the United States, demonstrate excellent agreement and highlight the critical role of memory and stochasticity in shaping infection trajectories. The results underscore the utility of fractal-fractional stochastic modeling for improving predictive power and informing targeted intervention strategies in emerging epidemics.

Limitations and evidence strength

猴痘 作为急性扁桃体炎的非典型表现:来自阿拉伯联合酋长国的 病例报告

Atypical Presentation of Mpox as Acute Tonsillitis: A Case Report from the United Arab Emirates
作者： Sara Omer Mohamed Omer, Mortada Sayedalhassan Yasen Osman, Ahmad Younes, Tamadur Maweya Abdelbagi Albasheer, Sunil Kumar Shivarame Gowda
摘要中文翻译猴痘 通常表现为发烧和淋巴结肿大的前驱症状,随后出现特征性水疱脓疱性皮疹。猴痘 可能出现非典型表现,包括孤立的口咽部、肛门生殖器和粘膜受累,先于皮肤病变。识别这些非典型表型对于避免诊断延迟和限制继发性 传播 至关重要,特别是在阿拉伯联合酋长国和更广泛的海湾等地区,这些地区已发表的非典型表现描述仍然有限。阿拉伯联合酋长国一名原本健康的 28 岁男性出现了两天严重喉咙痛和吞咽疼痛导致无法进食的病史。检查发现双侧腭扁桃体红斑,有隐秘渗出物,悬雍垂水肿,咽部红斑。没有喘鸣或呼吸困难。炎症标志物升高(WBC 计数 14.0 × 109/L;CRP 102 mg/L)。颈部计算机断层扫描显示腭扁桃体体积庞大、异质,口咽腔明显变窄,颈部淋巴结肿大;放射科医生的印象提出了发展脓肿的可能性。制作了急性细菌性扁桃体炎工作诊断,并开始静脉注射头孢曲松和地塞米松。住院第 2 天,出现轻度非特异性皮肤损害,第 3 天进展为特征性水疱脓疱疹,累及面部、躯干、手掌和生殖器区域。第 4 天完成的咽喉培养结果显示上呼吸道菌群正常,没有发现 β 溶血性链球菌。皮肤病变拭子的实时 PCR 证实了 猴痘病毒 感染(循环阈值 12)。该患者在入院第 9 天被隔离并转移至指定机构,并于 2026 年 1 月 11 日出院回家,距入院总共 27 天。2026 年 1 月 12 日进行的结构化出院后电话随访证实,患者持续临床康复,病变完全消退,并且没有复发症状。猴痘 最初可表现为严重的咽扁桃体炎,伴有明显的口咽腔狭窄,类似于急性细菌感染,并且在出现皮肤病变之前几天,即使是在没有公认的流行病学危险因素的年轻、免疫功能正常的患者中也是如此。临床医生应在患有严重咽扁桃体炎的患者中考虑使用猴痘,特别是当细菌培养呈阴性或入院期间甚至出现细微的皮肤变化时,以便及时隔离和适当的治疗。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在报告 猴痘 为急性扁桃体炎的非典型表现,以强调在对此类表现的公开描述有限的地区,诊断面临的挑战。
设计与对象
这是一位阿拉伯联合酋长国的 病例报告,他以前很健康,28 岁,患有严重的喉咙痛和吞咽疼痛。
核心方法
对患者进行了炎症标记物、颈部计算机断层扫描、咽喉培养以及皮损拭子实时 PCR 评估,以确认 猴痘病毒 感染。
主要结果
患者最初表现为双侧腭扁桃体红斑,有隐秘渗出物,炎症标志物升高,影像学提示脓肿;皮损出现较晚,PCR证实为猴痘,循环阈值为12。
解释与创新
作者解释说,猴痘 最初可以模仿急性细菌性扁桃体炎,即使是在没有危险因素的免疫功能正常的年轻患者中,并强调了识别非典型表现以避免诊断延误的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果告知临床医生在严重咽扁桃体炎病例中考虑 猴痘,尤其是细菌培养阴性或皮肤细微变化的病例,以便及时隔离和治疗。
局限与证据强度
尽管详细的临床和实验室研究结果为这种非典型表现提供了可靠的记录,但由于单例设计,证据强度较低,限制了普遍性。

Atypical Presentation of Mpox as Acute Tonsillitis: A Case Report from the United Arab Emirates

Authors: Sara Omer Mohamed Omer, Mortada Sayedalhassan Yasen Osman, Ahmad Younes, Tamadur Maweya Abdelbagi Albasheer, Sunil Kumar Shivarame Gowda
Original abstract (English)
Mpox typically presents with a prodrome of fever and lymphadenopathy followed by a characteristic vesiculopustular rash. Mpox may present with atypical manifestations, including isolated oropharyngeal, anogenital, and mucosal involvement preceding cutaneous lesions. Recognition of these atypical phenotypes is essential to avoid diagnostic delay and limit secondary transmission, particularly in regions such as the United Arab Emirates and the wider Gulf where published descriptions of atypical presentations remain limited. A previously healthy 28-year-old man in the United Arab Emirates presented with a two-day history of severe sore throat and painful swallowing that prevented oral intake. Examination revealed bilateral erythematous palatine tonsils with cryptic exudate, an edematous uvula, and pharyngeal erythema. There was no stridor or respiratory distress. Inflammatory markers were elevated (WBC count 14.0 × 109/L; CRP 102 mg/L). Computed tomography of the neck demonstrated bulky, heterogeneous palatine tonsils with significant narrowing of the oropharyngeal lumen and reactive cervical lymphadenopathy; the radiologist’s impression raised the possibility of evolving abscess. A working diagnosis of acute bacterial tonsillitis was made, and intravenous ceftriaxone and dexamethasone were commenced. On hospital day 2, mild non-specific cutaneous lesions appeared, progressing on day 3 to a characteristic vesiculopustular eruption involving the face, trunk, palms, and genital area. Throat culture, finalised on day 4, yielded normal upper respiratory tract flora with no beta-haemolytic streptococci. Real-time PCR on a skin lesion swab confirmed Mpox virus infection (cycle threshold 12). The patient was isolated, transferred to a designated facility on hospital day 9, and discharged home on 11 January 2026 after a total of 27 days from initial admission. A structured post-discharge telephone follow-up on 12 January 2026 confirmed sustained clinical recovery with full lesion resolution and no recurrent symptoms. Mpox can present initially as severe pharyngotonsillitis with significant oropharyngeal luminal narrowing, mimicking acute bacterial infection and preceding the appearance of skin lesions by several days, even in young, immunocompetent patients without recognised epidemiological risk factors. Clinicians should consider Mpox in patients with severe pharyngotonsillitis, particularly when bacterial cultures are negative or when even subtle cutaneous changes appear during admission, to enable timely isolation and appropriate management.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to report an atypical presentation of mpox as acute tonsillitis to highlight diagnostic challenges in regions with limited published descriptions of such manifestations.
Design and population
This was a case report of a previously healthy 28-year-old man in the United Arab Emirates who presented with severe sore throat and painful swallowing.
Core methods
The patient was evaluated with inflammatory markers, computed tomography of the neck, throat culture, and real-time PCR on a skin lesion swab to confirm mpox virus infection.
Main results
The patient initially presented with bilateral erythematous palatine tonsils with cryptic exudate, elevated inflammatory markers, and imaging suggestive of abscess; skin lesions appeared later, and PCR confirmed mpox with a cycle threshold of 12.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that mpox can initially mimic acute bacterial tonsillitis, even in young immunocompetent patients without risk factors, and emphasized the importance of recognizing atypical presentations to avoid diagnostic delay.
Scientific and public-health significance
Findings inform clinicians to consider mpox in severe pharyngotonsillitis cases, especially with negative bacterial cultures or subtle cutaneous changes, to enable timely isolation and management.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is low due to the single-case design, which limits generalizability, though the detailed clinical and laboratory findings provide robust documentation of this atypical presentation.

2022 年 6 月至 2024 年 2 月,德国 猴痘 暴发 期间的 疫苗 安全 监测

Vaccine safety surveillance during an mpox outbreak, Germany, June 2022 to February 2024
作者： Doris Oberle, Alexandra Hofmann, Klaus Jansen, Uwe Koppe, Raskit Lachmann, Renz Streit
摘要中文翻译背景 2022 年 5 月,德国发现了第一例 猴痘 进化枝 IIb 病例,MVA-BN 疫苗 从 2022 年 6 月起上市。自2022年6月起,疫苗接种(STIKO)常务委员会推荐疫苗接种用于遏制猴痘病毒感染和预防疾病。目的 我们的主要目的是在 2022 年 6 月至 2024 年 2 月在德国推出 疫苗接种 活动后评估 猴痘 疫苗 Jynneos(改良版痘苗安卡拉、巴伐利亚-北欧、MVA-BN)的安全性;第二个目的是描述 猴痘 案例和 疫苗接种。方法 为了识别免疫接种后出现不良事件 (AEFI) 的病例和人员,我们使用了现有的 监测 系统。2022 年 6 月实施了自愿系统来监测 猴痘 疫苗接种。我们对病例、疫苗接种、个例安全报告 (ICSR) 和 AEFI 进行了描述性分析。结果 截至 2024 年 2 月 29 日,共通报了 3,807 起 猴痘 案件(数据检索于 2024 年 7 月)。根据自愿猴痘 疫苗接种监测,总共施用了78,739剂猴痘 疫苗剂量的Jynneos。对国家 AEFI 数据库的检索发现了 32 个 ICSR,其中 7 个是严重的。总共报告了 87 例 AEFI,其中大多数是通常与 疫苗接种 相关的局部或全身反应。16 个联邦州之一的 疫苗 使用缺失信息调整后的总体不良事件报告率为每 100,000 剂 98.7 例不良事件。结论 MVA-BN 疫苗 的安全性符合产品特性总结。没有发现新的安全信号。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
鉴于 2022 年 5 月检测到第一批 猴痘 clade IIb 病例以及 MVA-BN 疫苗 从 2022 年 6 月起开始上市,需要开展这项研究来评估 猴痘 疫苗 Jynneos 在德国推出后的安全性。
设计与对象
使用现有的 监测 系统对 2022 年 6 月至 2024 年 2 月期间德国的 猴痘 病例和 疫苗接种 病例进行描述性分析。
核心方法
该研究使用现有的 监测 系统来识别免疫接种后出现不良事件 (AEFI) 的病例和人员,并对病例、疫苗接种、个体病例安全报告 (ICSR) 和 AEFI 进行描述性分析。
主要结果
截至2024年2月29日,共报告了3,807例猴痘病例,服用了78,739剂猴痘 疫苗剂量的Jynneos,报告了87例AEFI,其中大多数是通常与疫苗接种相关的局部或全身反应。
解释与创新
作者解释说,MVA-BN 疫苗 的安全概况与产品特性总结一致,没有发现新的安全信号。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为 猴痘 疫苗接种 活动的安全监测提供了信息,支持在 猴痘 暴发 的公共卫生应对中继续使用 Jynneos。
局限与证据强度
由于对自愿 监测 系统的依赖以及 AEFI 可能漏报,证据强度中等,特别是在缺少 疫苗 使用信息的 16 个联邦州之一。

Vaccine safety surveillance during an mpox outbreak, Germany, June 2022 to February 2024

Authors: Doris Oberle, Alexandra Hofmann, Klaus Jansen, Uwe Koppe, Raskit Lachmann, Renz Streit
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND In May 2022, first cases of mpox clade IIb were detected in Germany, and MVA-BN vaccine was available from June 2022 onwards. Vaccination has been recommended by the Standing Committee on Vaccination (STIKO) since June 2022 to contain monkeypox virus infection and prevent disease. AIM Our primary aim was to evaluate the safety of the mpox vaccine Jynneos (modified Vaccinia Ankara, Bavaria-Nordic, MVA-BN) after the rollout of the vaccination campaign in Germany from June 2022 to February 2024; a secondary aim was to describe mpox cases and vaccinations. METHODS To identify cases and persons with adverse events following immunisation (AEFIs), we used existing surveillance systems. A voluntary system was implemented in June 2022 to monitor mpox vaccination. We performed a descriptive analysis of cases, vaccinations, individual case safety reports (ICSRs) and AEFIs. RESULTS Until 29 February 2024, 3,807 mpox cases were notified (data retrieved July 2024). According to the voluntary mpox vaccination monitoring, a total of 78,739 mpox vaccine doses of Jynneos were administered. A search of the national AEFI database identified 32 ICSRs, of which seven were serious. A total of 87 AEFIs were reported, most of which were local or systemic reactions that are generally associated with vaccination. The overall adverse event reporting rate adjusted for missing information on vaccine utilisation in one of 16 federal states was 98.7 adverse events per 100,000 doses. CONCLUSION The safety profile of the MVA-BN vaccine was in line with the summary of product characteristics. No new safety signals were identified.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was needed to evaluate the safety of the mpox vaccine Jynneos after its rollout in Germany, given the first cases of mpox clade IIb detected in May 2022 and the availability of the MVA-BN vaccine from June 2022 onwards.
Design and population
A descriptive analysis was conducted using existing surveillance systems to monitor mpox cases and vaccinations in Germany from June 2022 to February 2024.
Core methods
The study used existing surveillance systems to identify cases and persons with adverse events following immunisation (AEFIs), and performed a descriptive analysis of cases, vaccinations, individual case safety reports (ICSRs) and AEFIs.
Main results
Until 29 February 2024, 3,807 mpox cases were notified, 78,739 mpox vaccine doses of Jynneos were administered, and 87 AEFIs were reported, most of which were local or systemic reactions generally associated with vaccination.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the safety profile of the MVA-BN vaccine was in line with the summary of product characteristics, and no new safety signals were identified.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the safety monitoring of mpox vaccination campaigns, supporting the continued use of Jynneos in public health responses to mpox outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the reliance on a voluntary surveillance system and potential underreporting of AEFIs, particularly in one of 16 federal states with missing information on vaccine utilisation.

支链 C7 取代的 7-Deaza-SAH 类似物占据 猴痘病毒 VP39 和登革热病毒 NS5 甲基转移酶的整个 SAM 结合袋

Branched C7-Substituted 7-Deaza-SAH Analogues Occupy the Entire SAM-Binding Pocket of Mpox Virus VP39 and Dengue Virus NS5 Methyltransferases
作者： Milan Štefek, Martin Klima, Tomáš Otava, Dominika Chalupska, Milan Dejmek, Radim Nencka, Evzen Boura, Milan Stefek, Martin Klíma, Dominika Chalupská
摘要中文翻译摘要 病毒 RNA 帽 MTase 是 抗病毒药物 药物开发的有吸引力的靶标。我们之前发现 C7 取代的 7-脱氮-SAH 类似物是 猴痘病毒 2'-O-MTase VP39 的有效抑制剂。在这里,我们使用结构引导设计来开发支链 C7 取代的类似物,旨在接合 VP39 SAM 结合口袋的多个疏水区域。使用生物化学和晶体学方法对合成的化合物进行了表征。几种类似物可有效抑制 VP39,其中最有效的化合物的 IC 50 在数十纳摩尔范围内。VP39 与 STM1187 和 STM1189 复合的晶体结构证实支链芳香族取代基向 SAM 结合位点附近的疏水区域延伸。精确的配体构象受到连接体几何形状和支化基团连接模式的强烈影响。STM1078 还以亚微摩尔浓度抑制 DENV3 NS5 MTase,该复合物的结构揭示了 SAH 样核心的保守结合模式以及支链取代基的构象适应性。这些结果证明了从 7-脱氮位置进行三维扩展如何产生能够结合结构不同的病毒 MTase 的有效抑制剂。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
病毒 RNA 帽 MTase 是 抗病毒药物 药物开发的有吸引力的靶点。该研究旨在探索支链 C7 取代的 7-deaza-SAH 类似物作为 猴痘病毒 2'-O-MTase VP39 抑制剂的潜力。
设计与对象
该研究重点关注 猴痘病毒 及其 2′-O-MTase VP39。它涉及支链 C7 取代类似物的开发,旨在接合 VP39 SAM 结合口袋的多个疏水区域。
核心方法
该研究采用结构导向设计来开发支链 C7 取代类似物。使用生化和晶体学方法对合成的化合物进行表征,以评估它们与 VP39 SAM 结合袋的相互作用。
主要结果
几种类似物可有效抑制 VP39,其中最有效的化合物的 IC50 在数十纳摩尔范围内。晶体结构证实支链芳族取代基向邻近 SAM 结合位点的疏水区域延伸。
解释与创新
这些发现证明了从 7-脱氮位置进行三维扩展如何产生能够结合结构不同的病毒 MTase 的有效抑制剂,突出了 抗病毒药物 药物开发的新方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为 猴痘病毒 MTase 的有效抑制剂的设计提供了见解,这可以为针对该酶的 抗病毒药物 疗法的未来研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅基于抽象证据,这限制了分析的深度。然而,这些结果得到了生化和晶体学数据的支持,提供了中等程度的置信度。

Branched C7-Substituted 7-Deaza-SAH Analogues Occupy the Entire SAM-Binding Pocket of Mpox Virus VP39 and Dengue Virus NS5 Methyltransferases

Authors: Milan Štefek, Martin Klima, Tomáš Otava, Dominika Chalupska, Milan Dejmek, Radim Nencka, Evzen Boura, Milan Stefek, Martin Klíma, Dominika Chalupská
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Viral RNA-cap MTases are attractive targets for antiviral drug development. We previously identified C7-substituted 7-deaza-SAH analogues as potent inhibitors of the mpox virus 2′-O-MTase VP39. Here, we used structure-guided design to develop branched C7-substituted analogues intended to engage multiple hydrophobic regions of the VP39 SAM-binding pocket. The synthesized compounds were characterized using biochemical and crystallographic approaches. Several analogues effectively inhibited VP39, with the most potent compound displaying an IC 50 in the tens-of-nanomolar range. Crystal structures of VP39 in complex with STM1187 and STM1189 confirmed that the branched aromatic substituents extend towards hydrophobic regions adjacent to the SAM binding site. The precise ligand conformations were strongly influenced by linker geometry and the branching group’s mode of attachment. STM1078 also inhibited DENV3 NS5 MTase with submicromolar potency, and the complex’s structure revealed a conserved binding mode of the SAH-like core accompanied by conformational adaptability of the branched substituent. These results demonstrate how three-dimensional expansion from the 7-deaza position can generate potent inhibitors capable of binding structurally distinct viral MTases.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Viral RNA-cap MTases are attractive targets for antiviral drug development. The study aimed to explore the potential of branched C7-substituted 7-deaza-SAH analogues as inhibitors of the mpox virus 2′-O-MTase VP39.
Design and population
The study focused on the mpox virus and its 2′-O-MTase VP39. It involved the development of branched C7-substituted analogues intended to engage multiple hydrophobic regions of the VP39 SAM-binding pocket.
Core methods
The study used structure-guided design to develop branched C7-substituted analogues. The synthesized compounds were characterized using biochemical and crystallographic approaches to assess their interaction with the VP39 SAM-binding pocket.
Main results
Several analogues effectively inhibited VP39, with the most potent compound displaying an IC50 in the tens-of-nanomolar range. Crystal structures confirmed that the branched aromatic substituents extend towards hydrophobic regions adjacent to the SAM binding site.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate how three-dimensional expansion from the 7-deaza position can generate potent inhibitors capable of binding structurally distinct viral MTases, highlighting a novel approach for antiviral drug development.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide insights into the design of potent inhibitors for mpox virus MTases, which could inform future research on antiviral therapies targeting this enzyme.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on abstract-only evidence, which limits the depth of analysis. However, the results are supported by biochemical and crystallographic data, providing a moderate level of confidence.

痘苗病毒磷脂酶 D 折叠核酸内切酶 K4 的高分辨率结构。

High-resolution structure of the vaccinia virus phospholipase D-fold endonuclease K4.
作者： Henri Gröger, Candice Trouba, Jade Barbaste, Guillaume Lacroix, Guy Schoehn, Wim P Burmeister, Nicolas Tarbouriech
摘要中文翻译痘苗病毒 (VACV) 是一种与 猴痘病毒 密切相关的 正痘病毒,猴痘病毒 于 2022 年在全球范围内启动了 暴发。痘病毒 基因组 两侧是短的反向互补发夹端粒,其特征是病毒复制所必需的不匹配碱基和插入。在此背景下,有人提出了晚期蛋白 K4 的作用。K4 存在于病毒颗粒中,显然不是必需的,并且与 VACV F13 蛋白一样具有磷脂酶 D (PLD) 折叠。它还与其最接近的同源物哺乳动物 PLD3 具有折叠和核酸酶活性。我们在 dsDNA 背景下建立了针对 ssDNA 和发夹环和气泡的核酸内切酶活性,而 RNA 具有抗切割能力。K4 的 2.4 Å 冷冻电镜结构显示出不寻常的八聚体组装,也存在于溶液中。在低浓度下,还存在与hPLD3相似的四聚体和二聚体。尽管具有核酸酶活性,但 K4 中的 C 末端延伸会阻断 DNA 结合袋。使用无活性突变体时,DNA 19mer 会同时结合到八聚体的 2 个位点上,并取代 C 末端。尽管 DNA 的活性和方向不同,但 DNA 结合使用与 hPLD3 5'-核酸外切酶相似的残基。K4 的作用以及通过观察到的自抑制对其活性的控制仍然是个谜。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
鉴于痘苗病毒磷脂酶 D 折叠核酸内切酶 K4 在病毒复制中的作用及其与 猴痘 等其他痘病毒的关系,该研究旨在研究痘苗病毒磷脂酶 D 折叠核酸内切酶 K4 的结构和功能。
设计与对象
该研究重点关注牛痘病毒蛋白 K4,利用冷冻电子显微镜和生化检测分析其结构和酶学特性。
核心方法
研究人员确定了 K4 的 2.4 Å 冷冻电镜结构,并评估了其针对单链 DNA、发夹环和双链 DNA 中的气泡的核酸内切酶活性,同时测试了对 RNA 切割的抵抗力。
主要结果
K4 对 ssDNA 和发夹环表现出核酸内切酶活性,但对 RNA 没有活性,其 2.4 Å 冷冻电镜结构揭示了一种不寻常的八聚体组装,较低浓度形成类似于 hPLD3 的四聚体和二聚体。
解释与创新
作者将 K4 的结构和活性解释为痘病毒蛋白中独一无二的,并指出它通过 C 末端延伸进行自动抑制,并且尽管活性不同,但其 DNA 结合机制类似于 hPLD3。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现促进了对痘病毒酶机制的理解,可能为针对 正痘病毒 保守结构特征的 抗病毒药物 策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
尽管 K4 的确切生物学作用及其调控仍然是个谜,但由于高分辨率结构数据和直接生化测定,该研究的证据强度很高。

High-resolution structure of the vaccinia virus phospholipase D-fold endonuclease K4.

Authors: Henri Gröger, Candice Trouba, Jade Barbaste, Guillaume Lacroix, Guy Schoehn, Wim P Burmeister, Nicolas Tarbouriech
Original abstract (English)
Vaccinia virus (VACV) is an orthopoxvirus closely related to mpox virus, which started global outbreaks in 2022. Poxvirus genomes are flanked by short, inverted complementary hairpin telomeres that feature mismatched bases and insertions essential for viral replication. In this context, a role of the late protein K4 has been proposed. K4 is present in the virion, is apparently non-essential and has a phospholipase D (PLD)-fold as has VACV F13 protein. It also shares fold and nuclease activity with its closest homologue, mammalian PLD3. We established an endonuclease activity against ssDNA and hairpin loops and bubbles in a dsDNA context while RNA is resistant to cleavage. The 2.4 Å cryo-EM structure of K4 shows an unusual octameric assembly, also present in solution. At low concentration, tetramers and dimers similar to the one of hPLD3 are also present. Despite its nuclease activity, in K4 a C-terminal extension blocks the DNA binding pockets. Using an inactive mutant, fortuitously, a DNA 19mer bound simultaneously to 2 sites of the octamer where it displaced the C-termini. DNA binding uses similar residues as the hPLD3 5'-exonuclease, despite different activities and orientations of the DNA. The role of K4 and the control of its activity by the observed auto-inhibition remain enigmatic.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the structure and function of the vaccinia virus phospholipase D-fold endonuclease K4, given its proposed role in viral replication and its relationship to other poxviruses like mpox.
Design and population
The study focused on the vaccinia virus protein K4, analyzing its structural and enzymatic properties using cryo-electron microscopy and biochemical assays.
Core methods
The researchers determined the 2.4 Å cryo-EM structure of K4 and assessed its endonuclease activity against single-stranded DNA, hairpin loops, and bubbles in double-stranded DNA, while testing resistance to RNA cleavage.
Main results
K4 exhibits endonuclease activity against ssDNA and hairpin loops but not RNA, and its 2.4 Å cryo-EM structure reveals an unusual octameric assembly, with lower concentrations forming tetramers and dimers similar to hPLD3.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret K4's structure and activity as unique among poxvirus proteins, noting its auto-inhibition by a C-terminal extension and its DNA-binding mechanism resembling hPLD3 despite different activities.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings advance understanding of poxvirus enzymatic mechanisms, potentially informing antiviral strategies targeting conserved structural features across orthopoxviruses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence strength is high due to high-resolution structural data and direct biochemical assays, though the exact biological role of K4 and its regulation remain enigmatic.

使用分数阶时滞微分方程和 疫苗接种 控制对猴痘病进行数学分析

Mathematical Analysis of Monkeypox Disease Using Fractional Delay Differential Equations and Vaccination Control
作者： Jatin Bansal, Anoop Kumar, Aziz Khan, Khaled Naserallal, Thabet Abdeljawad, Rajermani Thinakaran
摘要中文翻译本研究提出了猴痘 传播 动力学的分数阶模型,该模型使用 Caputo 分数阶延迟微分方程制定。该模型包含两种类型的延迟:一种代表人类的诊断延迟,另一种与啮齿动物种群相关。提出的框架进一步整合了 疫苗接种 策略并评估临床护理的需求。解析上,解的存在性、唯一性、非负性和有界性已成立。基本再生数 R0 已得出,敏感性分析确定了对 R0 影响最大的参数。具体而言,传播 速率β1 和β3 以及参数ω1 的影响最强,而γ 的影响最小。此外,在存在时间延迟的情况下,使用曲线切换方法研究了无病平衡和地方病平衡的稳定性。此外,图形模拟还演示了延迟、疫苗接种 速率和分数阶的影响。结果表明,增加 疫苗接种 率会减少感染人口,并且与较低值的延迟相比,模型延迟参数的较大值会在固定时间产生较慢的疾病轨迹和较低的感染峰值。这反映了系统响应较慢,而不是实际情况。这些发现强调了疾病进展对延迟的敏感性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在开发一个数学模型来分析猴痘的 传播 动态并评估 疫苗接种 策略对疾病控制的影响。
设计与对象
该研究采用分数阶延迟微分方程模型来模拟猴痘 诊断 动力学,其中纳入了人类 诊断 和啮齿动物群体的延迟。该模型旨在评估 疫苗接种 率和分数阶对疾病进展的影响。
核心方法
该模型采用 Caputo 分数阶时滞微分方程制定,结合 疫苗接种 策略和临床护理需求。进行敏感性分析以确定影响基本再生数R0的参数。
主要结果
解的存在性、唯一性、非负性、有界性成立。推导出基本再生数R0,敏感性分析表明,传播速率β1和β3以及参数ω1对R0的影响最大,而γ影响最小。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,增加 疫苗接种 率可以减少感染人群,并且该模型强调了疾病进展对延迟的敏感性,为理解猴痘动态提供了一个新的框架。
科研与公卫意义
结果可以通过强调 疫苗接种 的重要性以及延迟在疾病 传播 中的作用,为公共卫生策略提供信息,为 监测 和控制措施提供见解。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于数学模型,不涉及经验数据,这可能会限制其对现实场景的适用性。该证据是理论证据,来自模拟,表明可信度中等。

Mathematical Analysis of Monkeypox Disease Using Fractional Delay Differential Equations and Vaccination Control

Authors: Jatin Bansal, Anoop Kumar, Aziz Khan, Khaled Naserallal, Thabet Abdeljawad, Rajermani Thinakaran
Original abstract (English)
This study presents a fractional-order model for the transmission dynamics of Monkeypox, formulated using Caputo fractional order delay differential equations. The model incorporates two types of delays: one representing diagnostic delays in humans and the other associated with rodent populations. The proposed framework further integrates vaccination strategies and assesses the demand for clinical care. Analytically, the existence, uniqueness, non-negativity, and boundedness of solutions have been established. The basic reproduction number R0 has been derived, and a sensitivity analysis identifies the parameters most influencing R0. Specifically, the transmission rates β1 and β3, and the parameter ω1 exhibit the strongest influence, whereas the γ has the least impact. Furthermore, the stability of the disease-free and endemic equilibria has been investigated using the curve-switching method in the presence of time delays. Additionally, graphical simulations demonstrated the effects of delays, vaccination rates, and fractional orders. The results indicate that increasing the vaccination rate reduces the infected population, and large values of the delay parameters of the model produce slower disease trajectories and lower infection peaks at fixed time as compared to delays with lower values. This reflects a slower system response rather than the actual situation. These findings highlight the sensitivity of disease progression to delays.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to develop a mathematical model to analyze the transmission dynamics of Monkeypox and evaluate the impact of vaccination strategies on disease control.
Design and population
The study employed a fractional-order delay differential equations model to simulate Monkeypox transmission dynamics, incorporating delays in human diagnosis and rodent populations. The model was designed to assess the effects of vaccination rates and fractional orders on disease progression.
Core methods
The model was formulated using Caputo fractional order delay differential equations, integrating vaccination strategies and clinical care demand. Sensitivity analysis was conducted to identify parameters influencing the basic reproduction number R0.
Main results
The existence, uniqueness, non-negativity, and boundedness of solutions were established. The basic reproduction number R0 was derived, and sensitivity analysis showed that transmission rates β1 and β3, and parameter ω1 have the strongest influence on R0, while γ has the least impact.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that increasing vaccination rates reduces the infected population, and the model highlights the sensitivity of disease progression to delays, providing a novel framework for understanding Monkeypox dynamics.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results can inform public health strategies by emphasizing the importance of vaccination and the role of delays in disease transmission, offering insights for surveillance and control measures.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on a mathematical model and does not involve empirical data, which may limit its applicability to real-world scenarios. The evidence is theoretical and derived from simulations, indicating moderate confidence.

2026 年 6 月,北爱尔兰,同性恋、双性恋和其他与严重直肠炎相关的男男性行为者中,Ib 猴痘 分支的 暴发 迅速发展

A rapidly developing outbreak of clade Ib mpox among gay, bisexual and other men who have sex with men associated with severe proctitis, Northern Ireland, June 2026
作者： Abbie Harrison, Catherine Burns, Corey Magee, Monica Sloan, Joy Murphy, Laura McCartney, Maeve Middleton, Emma McCarty, Eoin Walker, John White
摘要中文翻译我们描述了北爱尔兰同性恋、双性恋和其他男男性行为者中快速扩张的 Ib 猴痘 暴发 分支。在 12 个月没有发现 猴痘 病例的情况下,2026 年 6 月 6 日至 30 日期间通报了 22 例确诊病例。病例的中位年龄为 41 岁;19 人在症状出现前 21 天内没有离开过英国或爱尔兰。暴发 的特点是严重的肛门直肠疾病,并需要接受非性健康环境,包括二级护理。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这项研究旨在描述北爱尔兰同性恋、双性恋和其他男男性行为者中快速扩张的 Ib 猴痘 暴发 分支,强调需要了解这一特定分支在特定人群中的出现。
设计与对象
该研究是一项描述性的 暴发 调查,于 2026 年 6 月 6 日至 30 日在北爱尔兰进行,重点关注同性恋、双性恋和其他男男性行为者中的 22 例确诊病例,中位年龄为 41 岁。
核心方法
调查涉及病例通报、人口统计数据收集以及 暴发 特征分析,包括识别严重肛门直肠疾病和非性健康环境作为呈现地点。
主要结果
暴发的特点是严重的肛门直肠疾病,2026年6月6日至30日期间报告了22例确诊病例,其中19例在症状出现前21天内没有英国或爱尔兰境外旅行史。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为表明 Ib 猴痘 分支具有独特的 暴发 模式,与严重的肛门直肠疾病和非性健康环境显着相关,这是在该人群中的一个新观察结果。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为高危人群(特别是具有性健康相关风险因素的人群)中针对 Ib 猴痘 分支的针对性 监测 和公共卫生应对措施提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于基于病例通报的描述性分析,由于缺乏详细的统计或因果分析,证据不充分。

A rapidly developing outbreak of clade Ib mpox among gay, bisexual and other men who have sex with men associated with severe proctitis, Northern Ireland, June 2026

Authors: Abbie Harrison, Catherine Burns, Corey Magee, Monica Sloan, Joy Murphy, Laura McCartney, Maeve Middleton, Emma McCarty, Eoin Walker, John White
Original abstract (English)
We describe a rapidly expanding clade Ib mpox outbreak among gay, bisexual and other men who have sex with men in Northern Ireland. Following 12 months with no detected mpox cases, 22 confirmed cases were notified between 6 and 30 June 2026. Cases had a median age of 41 years; 19 had not travelled outside the United Kingdom or Ireland in the 21 days before symptom onset. The outbreak is characterised by severe anorectal disease and presentation to non-sexual health settings, including secondary care.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to describe a rapidly expanding clade Ib mpox outbreak among gay, bisexual and other men who have sex with men in Northern Ireland, highlighting the need to understand the emergence of this specific clade in a particular population.
Design and population
The study is a descriptive outbreak investigation conducted in Northern Ireland between 6 and 30 June 2026, focusing on 22 confirmed cases among gay, bisexual and other men who have sex with men, with a median age of 41 years.
Core methods
The investigation involved case notification, demographic data collection, and analysis of the outbreak's characteristics, including the identification of severe anorectal disease and non-sexual health settings as presentation sites.
Main results
The outbreak was characterised by severe anorectal disease, with 22 confirmed cases reported between 6 and 30 June 2026, and 19 cases had no travel history outside the United Kingdom or Ireland in the 21 days before symptom onset.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as indicating a unique outbreak pattern of clade Ib mpox, with a notable association with severe anorectal disease and non-sexual health settings, which is a novel observation in this population.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted surveillance and public health responses for clade Ib mpox in at-risk populations, particularly those with sexual health-related risk factors.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to descriptive analysis based on case notifications, and the evidence is moderate due to the lack of detailed statistical or causal analyses.

烧蚀点阵 CO2 激光治疗 猴痘 后面部疤痕

Ablative Fractional CO2 Laser Therapy for a Post-Mpox Facial Scar
作者： Justyna Czarny-Kamm, Damian Kadylak, Monika Sikorska, Roman J. Nowicki, R. Nowicki
摘要中文翻译电子邮件:damian.kadylak@gumed.edu.pl 简介 猴痘(以前称为猴痘)可导致残留疤痕,特别是在可见或功能敏感区域(例如面部)。最后一次疗程后六个月,患者保持了良好的美学效果,朱红色边缘变形显着减少,患者报告的结果令人满意。描述了结合脉冲染料和 1550 nm Er: 玻璃激光 治疗 治疗阴茎疤痕,取得了良好的结果 [4],而 Raffaele 等人。结论 烧蚀点阵 CO2 激光治疗可能提供一种有效的方法来改善 猴痘 术后疤痕的美容和功能结果。猴痘感染后疤痕后遗症的前瞻性观察研究:40例患者的分析。研究信| Dermatol 实践概念。2026;16(3):7149 1 消融点阵 CO2 激光治疗 猴痘 后面部疤痕 Justyna Czarny-Kamm1,2,Damian Kadylak1,2,Monika Sikorska1,2,Roman J. Nowicki1,2 1 美国医科大学医学院皮肤病学、性病学和过敏学系格但斯克,格但斯克,波兰 2 皮肤病学、性病学和过敏学系,大学临床中心,格但斯克,波兰 关键词:猴痘、CO2 激光治疗、面部疤痕 引用:Czarny-Kamm J、Kadylak D、Sikorska M、Nowicki RJ。烧蚀点阵 CO2 激光治疗 猴痘 后面部疤痕。Dermatol 实践概念。这是一篇根据知识共享署名非商业许可 (BY-NC-4.0) 条款分发的开放获取文章,https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-nc/4.0/,允许在任何媒体上不受限制地非商业使用、分发和复制,前提是注明原始作者和来源。竞争利益:无。作者身份:所有作者都对此做出了重大贡献...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究报告了 猴痘 消融点阵 CO2 激光治疗的成功案例,解决了已发表的关于处理 猴痘 感染残留疤痕的有限数据,特别是在面部等可见区域。
设计与对象
这是一名 28 岁白人男性的 病例报告,他在 猴痘 感染后面部留下了广泛的疤痕,每月接受四次消融点阵 CO2 激光治疗。
核心方法
患者接受了四次消融点阵 CO2 激光治疗(功率 20 W;停留时间 3000 μs;重复间隔 0.5 s),并进行 治疗 后护理,包括润肤剂和广谱光保护。
主要结果
最后一次疗程后六个月,患者保持了良好的美学效果,朱红边界变形显着减少,患者报告的结果令人满意,没有感染、长时间红斑或色素沉着等并发症。
解释与创新
作者将烧蚀点阵 CO2 激光疗法解释为治疗 猴痘 后广泛疤痕的一种安全、有效和微创的选择,支持其作为标准化 治疗 方法的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,烧蚀点阵 CO2 激光疗法可以改善 猴痘 后疤痕的美容和功能结果,为新兴的皮肤病学和重建挑战提供潜在的解决方案。
局限与证据强度
猴痘 诊断的单例设计和缺乏实验室确认,证据强度有限;需要进一步的研究来建立标准化的 治疗 参数并评估长期结果。

Ablative Fractional CO2 Laser Therapy for a Post-Mpox Facial Scar

Authors: Justyna Czarny-Kamm, Damian Kadylak, Monika Sikorska, Roman J. Nowicki, R. Nowicki
Original abstract (English)
E-mail: damian.kadylak@ gumed.edu.pl Introduction Mpox (formerly monkeypox) can result in residual scarring, particularly in visible or functionally sensitive areas such as the face. Six months after the final session, the patient maintained a good aesthetic result with significantly reduced vermilion bor- der deformation and satisfactory patient-reported outcome. described combined pulsed-dye and 1550 nm Er:Glass laser treatment of a penile scar with ­favorable results [4], while Raffaele et al. Conclusion Ablative fractional CO2 laser therapy may offer an effective approach to improve both cosmetic and functional outcomes in post-Mpox scars. Research Letter | Dermatol Pract Concept. 2026;16(3):7149 1 Ablative Fractional CO2 Laser Therapy for a Post-Mpox Facial Scar Justyna Czarny-Kamm1,2, Damian Kadylak1,2, Monika Sikorska1,2, Roman J. Nowicki1,2 1 Department of Dermatology, Venereology and Allergology, Faculty of Medicine, Medical University of Gdańsk, Gdańsk, Poland 2 Department of Dermatology, Venereology and Allergology, University Clinical Centre, Gdańsk, Poland Key words: Mpox, CO2 Laser therapy, Facial scar Citation: Czarny-Kamm J, Kadylak D, Sikorska M, Nowicki RJ. View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the limited published data on managing residual scarring from Mpox infection, particularly in visible areas like the face, by reporting a case of successful treatment with ablative fractional CO2 laser therapy.
Design and population
This was a case report of a 28-year-old Caucasian male with extensive facial scarring following Mpox infection, treated with four sessions of ablative fractional CO2 laser therapy at monthly intervals.
Core methods
The patient underwent four sessions of ablative fractional CO2 laser therapy (power 20 W; dwell time 3000 μs; repeat interval 0.5 s) with post-treatment care including emollients and broad-spectrum photoprotection.
Main results
Six months after the final session, the patient maintained a good aesthetic result with significantly reduced vermilion border deformation and satisfactory patient-reported outcomes, with no complications such as infection, prolonged erythema, or dyspigmentation.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted ablative fractional CO2 laser therapy as a safe, effective, and minimally invasive option for extensive post-Mpox scarring, supporting its potential as a standardized treatment approach.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that ablative fractional CO2 laser therapy can improve both cosmetic and functional outcomes in post-Mpox scars, providing a potential solution for an emerging dermatological and reconstructive challenge.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is limited by the single-case design and lack of laboratory confirmation of Mpox diagnosis; further studies are required to establish standardized treatment parameters and evaluate long-term outcomes.

使用编码 I 型干扰素结合蛋白的 mRNA-LNP,在 疫苗接种 后快速防止致命的 正痘病毒 感染 2259797

Rapid protection from lethal Orthopoxvirus infection following vaccination with mRNA-LNP encoding the Type I Interferon Binding Protein 2259797
作者： Megan Keller, Norbert Pardi, Luis Sigal
摘要中文翻译摘要 简介 正痘病毒 (OPV),如猴痘,对公共卫生构成持续威胁,特别是在常规天花 疫苗接种 停止后,全球群体免疫力下降。当前的 疫苗 策略由于安全性和短暂的免疫力而存在局限性。大多数抗病毒 疫苗 旨在诱导与结构病毒蛋白结合的抗体 (Ab),优先是那些可以中和病毒颗粒的抗体。I 型干扰素结合蛋白 (IFN-I bp) 是一种高度保守的非结构蛋白,存在于所有 OPV 中,是一种免疫逃避蛋白,对于其致病性至关重要。此前已有研究表明,接种重组 IFN-I bp 疫苗或被动免疫抗 IFN-I bp 单克隆抗体 (Abs) 的小鼠可免受小鼠 OPV 痘痘病毒 (ECTV) 的致命攻击,ECTV 是人类天花和猴痘感染的经典模型。方法我们制备了编码IFN-I bp的mRNA-脂质纳米颗粒(mRNA-LNP)疫苗。我们通过 ELISA 评估了 Abs 反应,并通过在足垫中使用 ECTV 或鼻内使用 OPV 牛痘病毒攻击免疫小鼠来评估保护效果。结果我们发现,与对照 mRNA-LNP 疫苗 相比,编码 ECTV IFN-I bp 的 5 ug mRNA-LNP 的单次免疫早在免疫后六天就诱导了更高滴度的 IgG 抗体(而不是 IgM)。值得注意的是,接种 BALB/c 和 TLR9 缺陷的小鼠在免疫后八天接受 ECTV 或 VACV 攻击后,完全免受病毒致死作用。结论 这些数据表明 IFN-I bp 是 OPV 疫苗 的一种有前途的抗原,特别是在快速保护至关重要的情况下,例如在流行期间。正在进行的研究旨在确定快速 IgG 诱导的机制。资金来源拨款主题类别 疫苗 和免疫疗法 (VAC)
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
正痘病毒 (OPV),如猴痘,由于常规天花 疫苗接种 停止后全球群体免疫力下降,对公共卫生构成持续威胁,而当前的 疫苗 策略由于安全性和短期免疫力而存在局限性。
设计与对象
该研究评估了小鼠体内编码 I 型 IFN 结合蛋白的 mRNA-脂质纳米颗粒 (mRNA-LNP) 疫苗,通过 ELISA 评估抗体反应以及针对 ectromelia 病毒 (ECTV) 或牛痘病毒攻击的保护功效。
核心方法
研究人员开发了编码 IFN-I bp 的 mRNA-LNP 疫苗,通过 ELISA 测量 IgG 和 IgM 抗体滴度,并用足垫内的 ECTV 或鼻内痘苗病毒攻击免疫 BALB/c 和 TLR9 缺陷小鼠,以评估保护作用。
主要结果
与对照相比,用 5 μg 编码 ECTV IFN-I bp 的 mRNA-LNP 进行单次免疫,早在免疫后六天就诱导了更高的 IgG 抗体滴度,并且接种疫苗的小鼠在免疫后八天完全免受 ECTV 或牛痘病毒的致死攻击。
解释与创新
作者解释说,IFN-I bp 是 OPV 疫苗 的一种有前途的抗原,特别是当快速保护至关重要时,例如在流行病期间,免疫接种后不久观察到的全面保护就证明了这一点。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,针对 IFN-I bp 的 mRNA-LNP 疫苗 可以提供针对 正痘病毒 感染的快速保护,为快速免疫至关重要的流行病应对提供潜在策略。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究是在小鼠模型中进行的,并且重点关注有限数量的病毒挑战;需要进一步的研究来确认对人类的功效并了解快速 IgG 诱导的机制。

Rapid protection from lethal Orthopoxvirus infection following vaccination with mRNA-LNP encoding the Type I Interferon Binding Protein 2259797

Authors: Megan Keller, Norbert Pardi, Luis Sigal
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction Orthopoxviruses (OPVs), such as monkeypox, pose ongoing public health threats, especially since the global herd immunity has declined following the cessation of routine smallpox vaccination. Current vaccine strategies have limitations due to safety and short-lived immunity. Most anti-viral vaccines aim to induce antibodies (Abs) that bind to structural viral proteins, preferentially those that can neutralize the viral particle. The Type I IFN-binding protein (IFN-I bp) is a highly conserved non-structural protein present in all OPVs, an immune evasion protein, and is essential for their pathogenicity. It has been previously shown that mice vaccinated with recombinant IFN-I bp or passively immunized with anti-IFN-I bp monoclonal antibodies (Abs), are protected from lethal challenge with the mouse OPV ectromelia virus (ECTV), a classic model for human smallpox and monkeypox infections. Methods We made mRNA-lipid nanoparticle (mRNA-LNP) vaccine encoding IFN-I bp. We evaluated Abs responses by ELISA, and protective efficacy by challenging the immunized mice with ECTV in the footpad or with the OPV vaccinia virus intranasally. Results We found that a single immunization of 5 ug of mRNA-LNPs encoding ECTV’s IFN-I bp induced much higher titers of IgG antibodies (as opposed to IgM) as early as six days post-immunization than control mRNA-LNP vaccines. Notably, vaccinated BALB/c and TLR9-deficient mice challenged with ECTV or VACV eight days post-immunization were fully protected from viral lethality. Conclusion These data indicate that the IFN-I bp is a promising antigen for OPV vaccines, particularly when rapid protection is crucial, such as during an epidemic. Ongoing studies aim to determine the mechanisms underlying rapid IgG induction. Funding Source grant Topic Categories Vaccines and Immunotherapy (VAC)
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Orthopoxviruses (OPVs), such as monkeypox, pose ongoing public health threats due to declining global herd immunity following the cessation of routine smallpox vaccination, and current vaccine strategies have limitations due to safety and short-lived immunity.
Design and population
The study evaluated an mRNA-lipid nanoparticle (mRNA-LNP) vaccine encoding the Type I IFN-binding protein in mice, assessing antibody responses by ELISA and protective efficacy against challenge with ectromelia virus (ECTV) or vaccinia virus.
Core methods
The researchers developed an mRNA-LNP vaccine encoding IFN-I bp, measured IgG and IgM antibody titers by ELISA, and challenged immunized BALB/c and TLR9-deficient mice with ECTV in the footpad or vaccinia virus intranasally to assess protection.
Main results
A single immunization with 5 μg of mRNA-LNPs encoding ECTV's IFN-I bp induced higher IgG antibody titers as early as six days post-immunization compared to controls, and vaccinated mice were fully protected from lethal challenge with ECTV or vaccinia virus eight days post-immunization.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the IFN-I bp is a promising antigen for OPV vaccines, particularly when rapid protection is crucial, such as during an epidemic, as demonstrated by the full protection observed shortly after immunization.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that mRNA-LNP vaccines targeting IFN-I bp could provide rapid protection against orthopoxvirus infections, offering a potential strategy for epidemic response where swift immunity is essential.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the study was conducted in mouse models and focused on a limited number of viral challenges; further research is needed to confirm efficacy in humans and understand the mechanisms of rapid IgG induction.

HIV 感染者或有 HIV 感染风险的人群中的 免疫应答 至 MVA-BN 和既往天花 疫苗接种

Immune Response to MVA-BN and Prior Smallpox Vaccination in People with HIV or at Risk for HIV Acquisition
作者： Frank J Palella, Elizabeth A Thompson, Aruna Chandran, Shanshan Li, Panayampalli S Satheshkumar, Michael Augenbraun, Michael B. Townsend, Daniel Merenstein, Matthew J Mimiaga, Anjali Sharma
摘要中文翻译对于 MVA-BN 疫苗接种 (JYNNEOS) 在感染或未感染 HIV 的老年人中针对 猴痘 引起的血清学反应知之甚少。在这项研究中,我们测量了多中心艾滋病 队列研究 研究/妇女机构间 HIV 研究联合 队列研究 研究中选定的 HIV 感染者或有感染 HIV 风险的参与者 (n=114) 的 正痘病毒 抗体水平,这些参与者之前没有报告过 猴痘 病史。参与者通过问卷报告 MVA-BN 疫苗接种 历史。中位年龄为 64 岁,24.6% 为女性,46% 为 HIV 血清阳性。114 名参与者中有 20 名至少接受了一剂 MVA-BN。我们发现,无论 HIV 状态如何,MVA-BN 都能诱导持续的 IgG 水平,甚至长达一年。1973 年之前出生与较高的 IgG 相关。未接种 MVA-BN 疫苗的 HIV 感染者的 IgG 水平低于未感染 HIV 的患者。尽管受到小样本量的限制,我们的研究是第一个评估抗 正痘病毒 抗体的研究之一,特别是在易受感染的老年人群中,并按 HIV 状况进行分层。

问题与背景
关于MVA-BN 疫苗接种针对猴痘在患有或不患有HIV的老年人中引起的血清学反应知之甚少。
设计与对象
在这项研究中,我们测量了多中心艾滋病 队列研究 研究/妇女机构间 HIV 研究联合 队列研究 研究中选定的 HIV 感染者或有感染 HIV 风险的参与者 (n=114) 的 正痘病毒 抗体水平,这些参与者之前没有报告过 猴痘 病史。
核心方法
参与者通过问卷报告 MVA-BN 疫苗接种 历史。
主要结果
中位年龄为 64 岁,24.6% 为女性,46% 为 HIV 血清阳性。114 名参与者中有 20 名至少接受了一剂 MVA-BN。我们发现,无论 HIV 状态如何,MVA-BN 都能诱导持续的 IgG 水平,甚至长达一年。
解释与创新
1973 年之前出生与较高的 IgG 相关。未接种 MVA-BN 疫苗的 HIV 感染者的 IgG 水平低于未感染 HIV 的患者。
科研与公卫意义
该研究深入了解了弱势老年人群中的 免疫应答 到 MVA-BN 疫苗接种,这对于该群体的 疫苗接种 策略非常重要。
局限与证据强度
尽管受到小样本量的限制,我们的研究是第一个评估抗 正痘病毒 抗体的研究之一,特别是在易受感染的老年人群中,并按 HIV 状况进行分层。

Immune Response to MVA-BN and Prior Smallpox Vaccination in People with HIV or at Risk for HIV Acquisition

Authors: Frank J Palella, Elizabeth A Thompson, Aruna Chandran, Shanshan Li, Panayampalli S Satheshkumar, Michael Augenbraun, Michael B. Townsend, Daniel Merenstein, Matthew J Mimiaga, Anjali Sharma
Original abstract (English)
Little is known about the serologic response elicited by MVA-BN vaccination (JYNNEOS) against mpox in elderly individuals with or without HIV. In this study, we measured levels of antibody against orthopoxvirus in selected participants (n=114) with HIV or at risk for HIV acquisition, from the Multicenter AIDS Cohort Study/Women's Interagency HIV Study Combined Cohort Study, who had no prior reported history of mpox. Participants reported MVA-BN vaccination history via questionnaires. Median age was 64 years, 24.6% were female, and 46% were HIV-seropositive. Twenty out of 114 participants received at least one dose of MVA-BN. We found that MVA-BN induced sustained IgG levels regardless of HIV status even up to one year. Birth before 1973 was correlated with higher IgG. MVA-BN unvaccinated individuals with HIV had lower IgG than those without HIV. Although limited by small sample size, our study is among the first to assess anti-orthopoxvirus antibodies specifically in a vulnerable, older population and stratified by HIV status.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Little is known about the serologic response elicited by MVA-BN vaccination against mpox in elderly individuals with or without HIV.
Design and population
In this study, we measured levels of antibody against orthopoxvirus in selected participants (n=114) with HIV or at risk for HIV acquisition, from the Multicenter AIDS Cohort Study/Women's Interagency HIV Study Combined Cohort Study, who had no prior reported history of mpox.
Core methods
Participants reported MVA-BN vaccination history via questionnaires.
Main results
Median age was 64 years, 24.6% were female, and 46% were HIV-seropositive. Twenty out of 114 participants received at least one dose of MVA-BN. We found that MVA-BN induced sustained IgG levels regardless of HIV status even up to one year.
Interpretation and novelty
Birth before 1973 was correlated with higher IgG. MVA-BN unvaccinated individuals with HIV had lower IgG than those without HIV.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study provides insights into the immune response to MVA-BN vaccination in a vulnerable, older population, which is important for vaccination strategies in this group.
Limitations and evidence strength
Although limited by small sample size, our study is among the first to assess anti-orthopoxvirus antibodies specifically in a vulnerable, older population and stratified by HIV status.

检查非洲接受改良痘苗安卡拉 疫苗 (MpoxVax AFRIVAC) 的参与者的 免疫应答:一项前瞻性 队列研究 研究 - 方案。

Examining the immune response of participants receiving Modified Vaccinia Ankara vaccine in Africa (MpoxVax AFRIVAC): a prospective cohort study - protocol.
作者： Joanne Byrne, Winters Muttamba, Alain Balola Ntaboba, Bariki Mtafya, Mudarshiru Bbuye, Arthur Kalyebi Watelo, Elena Alvarez Barco, Ayleen Mufudza, Barnabas Bakamutumaho, Eoin R Feeney
摘要中文翻译简介:猴痘 仍然是一个持续的全球健康问题,由中部非洲持续存在的 I 暴发 分支驱动,该分支已造成大量发病率和 死亡率。尽管部署了改良痘苗安卡拉-巴伐利亚北欧 (MVA-BN) 疫苗,但有关 疫苗 诱导的 免疫应答 持久性的数据仍然有限,特别是在非洲人群、妇女和艾滋病毒感染者中,这凸显了对具体情况免疫原性数据的需求。方法和分析:猴痘-Vax AFRIVAC 是一项在乌干达、坦桑尼亚和刚果民主共和国 (DRC) 进行的前瞻性、多中心观察性 队列研究 研究。对符合 MVA-BN 猴痘 预防资格的成人或在入组前 28 天内接受第一剂的成人进行为期 48 周的随访。收集连续血液样本以量化结合抗体反应并评估其随着时间的推移的耐久性。主要结果是第 48 周时的平均几何 MVA-BN 特异性抗体滴度以及从第 6 周到第 48 周的抗体衰减率。次要结果包括中间时间点的抗体动力学、与 免疫应答 相关的因素、突破性感染和 疫苗 安全性。分析将使用描述性和纵向统计方法来评估 免疫应答 轨迹和相关的 宿主 因素,包括 HIV 状况、年龄和性别。道德与传播:该研究是根据赫尔辛基宣言进行的,在泛非临床试验注册中心(PACTR202601855406764)注册,出版物将按照国际医学期刊编辑委员会的建议进行。该研究已获得乌干达、刚果民主共和国和坦桑尼亚的伦理批准。乌干达的批准已获得乌干达病毒研究所研究和伦理委员会(GC/127/1062)和乌干达国家科学技术委员会(HS5575ES)的批准,刚果民主共和国的批准已获得卫生伦理机构委员会(UCB/CIES/PB/001/2025)的批准,坦桑尼亚的批准已获得国家医学研究所(NIMR/HQ/R.8a/Vol.IX/5187)的批准。试用注册号:PACTR202601855406764。

Examining the immune response of participants receiving Modified Vaccinia Ankara vaccine in Africa (MpoxVax AFRIVAC): a prospective cohort study - protocol.

Authors: Joanne Byrne, Winters Muttamba, Alain Balola Ntaboba, Bariki Mtafya, Mudarshiru Bbuye, Arthur Kalyebi Watelo, Elena Alvarez Barco, Ayleen Mufudza, Barnabas Bakamutumaho, Eoin R Feeney
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Mpox remains an ongoing global health concern, driven by an ongoing clade I outbreak in Central Africa, which has caused substantial morbidity and mortality. Despite deployment of the Modified Vaccinia Ankara-Bavarian Nordic (MVA-BN) vaccine, data on the durability of vaccine-induced immune responses remain limited, particularly in African populations, women and people with HIV, highlighting the need for context-specific immunogenicity data. METHODS AND ANALYSIS: Mpox-Vax AFRIVAC is a prospective, multicentre observational cohort study conducted in Uganda, Tanzania and the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC). Adults eligible for MVA-BN vaccination for mpox prevention or who received a first dose within 28 days prior to enrolment are followed for 48 weeks. Serial blood samples are collected to quantify binding antibody responses and assess their durability over time. The primary outcomes are mean geometric MVA-BN-specific antibody titres at week 48 and the rate of antibody decay from week 6 to week 48. Secondary outcomes include antibody kinetics at intermediate time points, factors associated with immune responses, breakthrough infection and vaccine safety. Analyses will use descriptive and longitudinal statistical methods to evaluate immune response trajectories and associated host factors, including HIV status, age and sex. ETHICS AND DISSEMINATION: The study is conducted in accordance with the Declaration of Helsinki, registered at the Pan African Clinical Trials Registry (PACTR202601855406764) and publications will be in accordance with the recommendations of the International Committee of Medical Journal Editors. The study has received ethical approvals in Uganda, DRC and Tanzania. Approval for Uganda has been obtained from Uganda Virus Research Institute Research and Ethics Committee (GC/127/1062) and Uganda National Council for Science and Technology (HS5575ES), for DRC from the Institutional Committee of Health ethics (UCB/CIES/PB/001/2025) and for Tanzania from the National Institute for Medical Research (NIMR/HQ/R.8a/Vol.IX/5187). TRIAL REGISTRATION NUMBER: PACTR202601855406764.

PregInPoxVac 母体方案:一项在刚果民主共和国评估 MVA-BN 疫苗 母体免疫原性和安全性的 3 期试验。

PregInPoxVac maternal protocol: a phase 3 trial evaluating maternal immunogenicity and safety of the MVA-BN vaccine in the Democratic Republic of the Congo.
作者： Paulina Morales Ruiz, Solange Milolo Tshilumba, Kirsten Maertens, Vivi Maketa, Primo Kimbulu, Felly Mitashi, Mahmoud Abu Azoum, Gustave Kasereka, Aurélie Bisumba, Maha Salloum
摘要中文翻译简介:猴痘 在刚果民主共和国 (DRC) 仍然流行,孕妇患严重疾病和不良后果的风险增加。WHO已呼吁提供猴痘 疫苗在高危人群中的数据,但尚无临床试验评估猴痘 疫苗在该人群中的安全性或免疫原性。改良痘苗安卡拉-巴伐利亚北欧 (MVA-BN) 疫苗 是第三代、高度减毒、非复制的活病毒 疫苗,在青少年和成人(包括免疫功能低下的个体)中表现出出色的安全性,预计在怀孕期间也是安全的。方法和分析:妊娠期和婴儿期 猴痘 疫苗 研究是一项 3 期开放标签试验,旨在评估 MVA-BN 疫苗 在刚果民主共和国博恩德孕妇和产后妇女中的安全性、反应原性和免疫原性,与 POX-MVA-045 研究的成人相比(NCT06549530) 位于刚果民主共和国和乌干达。共有 359 名孕妇(16-35 岁,怀孕中期或晚期)被招募并随机接受同源 MVA-BN 疫苗 方案,间隔 28 天,第一剂在怀孕期间妊娠 32 周前或产后 72 小时内给药(3:2 比例随机化)。另外一个非随机组将在确认 猴痘 暴露后接受暴露后预防。初步免疫原性分析将评估孕妇中和抗体滴度与非妊娠成人相比的非劣效性。初步安全性分析将比较孕妇的反应原性与成人中观察到的反应原性,以及孕妇的安全性与成人和产后立即接种疫苗的女性观察到的安全性。二次分析将评估孕妇和产后妇女与成人和彼此之间随时间变化的中和抗体滴度和总结合抗体浓度。伦理与传播:该试验整合了辅助护理途径、当地能力建设和共同开发研究工具的形成研讨会。获得了刚果民主共和国和比利时机构和国家审查委员会的伦理批准。结果将与当地社区、国家利益相关者和全球卫生机构分享。试用注册号:NCT06844500 和 PACTR202511506487215。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
猴痘 在刚果民主共和国 (DRC) 仍然流行,孕妇患严重疾病和不良后果的风险增加。WHO已呼吁提供猴痘 疫苗在高危人群中的数据,但尚无临床试验评估猴痘 疫苗在该人群中的安全性或免疫原性。
设计与对象
刚果民主共和国博恩代正在进行一项 3 期开放标签试验,以评估 MVA-BN 疫苗 在孕妇和产后妇女中的安全性、反应原性和免疫原性,与刚果民主共和国和乌干达 POX-MVA-045 研究的成人相比。共有 359 名孕妇(16-35 岁,怀孕中期或晚期)被招募并随机接受同源 MVA-BN 疫苗 方案,间隔 28 天。
核心方法
妊娠和婴儿期 猴痘 疫苗 研究是一项 3 期开放标签试验,旨在与刚果民主共和国和乌干达的 POX-MVA-045 研究 (NCT06549530) 的成人相比,评估 MVA-BN 疫苗 在刚果博恩代孕妇和产后妇女中的安全性、反应原性和免疫原性。
主要结果
初步免疫原性分析将评估孕妇中和抗体滴度与非妊娠成人相比的非劣效性。二次分析将评估孕妇和产后妇女与成人和彼此之间随时间变化的中和抗体滴度和总结合抗体浓度。
解释与创新
该研究提供了有关 MVA-BN 疫苗 在孕妇中的安全性和免疫原性的新数据,解决了针对这一高危人群开发 猴痘 疫苗 的关键知识差距。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为孕妇制定 猴痘 疫苗接种 策略提供信息,有助于改善刚果民主共和国和其他流行地区的 监测、预防和公共卫生应对措施。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其关注单一地理位置且缺乏长期随访数据而受到限制。证据是温和的,因为它基于仅摘要的报告。

PregInPoxVac maternal protocol: a phase 3 trial evaluating maternal immunogenicity and safety of the MVA-BN vaccine in the Democratic Republic of the Congo.

Authors: Paulina Morales Ruiz, Solange Milolo Tshilumba, Kirsten Maertens, Vivi Maketa, Primo Kimbulu, Felly Mitashi, Mahmoud Abu Azoum, Gustave Kasereka, Aurélie Bisumba, Maha Salloum
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Mpox remains endemic in the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC), with pregnant women at increased risk of severe disease and adverse outcomes. The WHO has called for data on mpox vaccination in high-risk groups, but no clinical trial has assessed the safety or immunogenicity of mpox vaccines in this population. The modified vaccinia Ankara-Bavarian Nordic (MVA-BN) vaccine, a third-generation, highly attenuated, non-replicating, live virus vaccine, has shown an excellent safety profile in adolescents and adults, including immunocompromised individuals, and is expected to be safe during pregnancy. METHODS AND ANALYSIS: Pregnancy & Infancy mPox Vaccine study is a phase 3, open-label trial that aims to evaluate the safety, reactogenicity and immunogenicity of the MVA-BN vaccine in pregnant and postpartum women in Boende, the DRC, compared with adults of the POX-MVA-045 study (NCT06549530) in DRC and Uganda. A total of 359 pregnant women (16-35 years old, in their second or third trimester of pregnancy) are being enrolled and randomised to receive the homologous MVA-BN vaccine regimen at a 28-day interval, with the first dose administered either before 32 weeks of gestation during pregnancy or within 72 hours postpartum (3:2 ratio randomisation). An additional, non-randomised group will receive post-exposure prophylaxis following confirmed mpox exposure. Primary immunogenicity analyses will assess the non-inferiority of neutralising antibody titres in pregnant women compared with those in non-pregnant adults. Primary safety analyses will compare reactogenicity in pregnant women to that observed in adults and safety in pregnant women to safety observed in adults and women vaccinated in the immediate postpartum period. Secondary analysis will evaluate neutralising antibody titres and total binding antibody concentrations over time for pregnant and postpartum women compared with adults and each other. ETHICS AND DISSEMINATION: The trial integrates ancillary care pathways, local capacity building and formative workshops that co-developed research tools. Ethical approvals were obtained from institutional and national review boards in the DRC and Belgium. Results will be shared with local communities, national stakeholders and global health agencies. TRIAL REGISTRATION NUMBER: NCT06844500 & PACTR202511506487215.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Mpox remains endemic in the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC), with pregnant women at increased risk of severe disease and adverse outcomes. The WHO has called for data on mpox vaccination in high-risk groups, but no clinical trial has assessed the safety or immunogenicity of mpox vaccines in this population.
Design and population
A phase 3, open-label trial is being conducted in Boende, the DRC, to evaluate the safety, reactogenicity and immunogenicity of the MVA-BN vaccine in pregnant and postpartum women compared with adults of the POX-MVA-045 study in DRC and Uganda. A total of 359 pregnant women (16-35 years old, in their second or third trimester of pregnancy) are being enrolled and randomised to receive the homologous MVA-BN vaccine regimen at a 28-day interval.
Core methods
The Pregnancy & Infancy mPox Vaccine study is a phase 3, open-label trial that aims to evaluate the safety, reactogenicity and immunogenicity of the MVA-BN vaccine in pregnant and postpartum women in Boende, the DRC, compared with adults of the POX-MVA-045 study (NCT06549530) in DRC and Uganda.
Main results
Primary immunogenicity analyses will assess the non-inferiority of neutralising antibody titres in pregnant women compared with those in non-pregnant adults. Secondary analysis will evaluate neutralising antibody titres and total binding antibody concentrations over time for pregnant and postpartum women compared with adults and each other.
Interpretation and novelty
The study provides novel data on the safety and immunogenicity of the MVA-BN vaccine in pregnant women, addressing a critical knowledge gap in mpox vaccine development for this high-risk group.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of mpox vaccination strategies for pregnant women, contributing to improved surveillance, prevention and public health responses in the DRC and other endemic regions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on a single geographic location and the absence of long-term follow-up data. The evidence is moderate as it is based on an abstract-only report.

暴发 低收入和中等收入国家妇女和女童的准备:一项定性研究。

Outbreak preparedness for women and girls in low- and middle-income countries: a qualitative study.
作者： Danielle Sarraf, Melissa Meinhart, Ilana Seff, Catherine Poulton, Sarah Karmin, Christine Heckman, Lindsay Stark
摘要中文翻译背景:传染病 暴发(“暴发”)给妇女和女童带来更高的性别暴力 (GBV) 风险。然而,人们对将 GBV 和性别知情规划纳入低收入和中等收入国家 (LMIC) 的 暴发 准备和响应中的情况知之甚少。方法:这项定性研究借鉴了对 10 名公共卫生从业者的深入访谈,他们具有支持中低收入国家传染病 暴发 准备和应对的经验。参与者代表政府间组织,并在多个 暴发 背景下工作,包括埃博拉、COVID-19、猴痘、寨卡和霍乱,在不同的地理环境中。访谈考察了将性别知情规划和 GBV 考虑因素纳入 暴发 准备和响应的障碍、差距、成功和机会。作者采用主题内容分析和混合归纳和演绎编码方法来分析数据。结果:出现了四个主要主题。首先,与会者描述了在优先事项相互竞争和资金不足的推动下,将性别和 GBV 考虑因素排除在 暴发 响应之外的持续正常化。其次,他们发现社区参与和本地化不足,强调应对措施往往未能考虑到现有的性别规范、社区优先事项和当地专业知识。第三,与会者强调了跨部门和组织的分散协调,主张与女性领导的组织(WLO)建立更强有力的伙伴关系,改善协作,并实现更加性别平衡的领导力。最后,他们强调需要更好地将经验教训制度化并运用现有证据,以加强准备工作并支持在未来的 暴发 期间采取更有效、性别信息丰富的应对措施。结论:为了加强全球 暴发 应对措施中的性别融合,我们建议将 GBV 风险缓解纳入整个应对计划中,改善跨部门协调和性别平衡领导力,加强与当地 WLO 的伙伴关系,并采用灵活的长期融资机制。将性别知情方法纳入 暴发 准备工作对于保护妇女和女童、增强社区复原力以及提高中低收入国家公共卫生应对措施的有效性和可持续性至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了中低收入国家 (LMIC) 在将基于性别的暴力 (GBV) 和性别知情规划纳入 暴发 准备和应对工作中持续存在的差距。它强调了妇女和女童在传染病 暴发 期间面临的更大的 GBV 风险,这是一个关键的公共卫生问题,需要有针对性的准备策略。
设计与对象
这项定性研究对 10 名在支持中低收入国家传染病 暴发 准备和应对方面经验丰富的公共卫生从业者进行了深入访谈。参与者代表政府间组织,并在多个 暴发 背景下工作,包括埃博拉、COVID-19、猴痘、寨卡和霍乱,在不同的地理环境中。
核心方法
作者采用主题内容分析,使用归纳和演绎混合编码方法来分析访谈数据。访谈旨在检查将性别知情规划和 GBV 考虑因素纳入 暴发 准备和响应的障碍、差距、成功和机会。
主要结果
访谈中出现了四个主要主题:由于优先事项相互竞争和资金不足,持续排除性别和 GBV 考虑因素;社区参与和本地化不足;跨部门协调分散;以及需要将所吸取的经验教训制度化并运用现有证据以做好未来的准备。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解读为证据,表明 暴发 应对措施中的性别融合仍然没有得到优先重视,尽管它在保护妇女和女童以及增强社区复原力方面发挥着关键作用。他们强调以从业者的观点为中心来确定可操作的差距和系统改进机会的新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,将缓解性别暴力风险纳入应对计划、改善跨部门协调、加强与妇女领导的组织的伙伴关系以及采用灵活的长期融资机制,可以提高中低收入国家公共卫生应对措施的有效性和可持续性。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其样本量较小,只有十名从业者,并且依赖定性方法,可能无法捕捉到不同背景下的全部障碍或成功。由于缺乏定量验证或更广泛的利益相关者视角,证据强度中等。

Outbreak preparedness for women and girls in low- and middle-income countries: a qualitative study.

Authors: Danielle Sarraf, Melissa Meinhart, Ilana Seff, Catherine Poulton, Sarah Karmin, Christine Heckman, Lindsay Stark
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Infectious disease outbreaks ("outbreaks") pose heightened gender-based violence (GBV) risk for women and girls. However, little is known about the integration of GBV and gender-informed programming within outbreak preparedness and response in low- and middle-income countries (LMICs). METHODS: This qualitative study draws on in-depth interviews with ten public health practitioners with experience supporting infectious disease outbreak preparedness and response in LMICs. Participants represented intergovernmental organizations and worked across multiple outbreak contexts, including Ebola, COVID-19, Mpox, Zika, and Cholera, in diverse geographic settings. Interviews examined barriers, gaps, successes, and opportunities for integrating gender-informed programming and GBV considerations into outbreak preparedness and response. Authors employed thematic content analysis with a mixed inductive and deductive coding approach to analyze data. RESULTS: Four major themes emerged. First, participants described the persistent normalization of excluding gender and GBV considerations from outbreak response, driven by competing priorities and inadequate funding. Second, they identified insufficient community engagement and localization, emphasizing that responses often fail to account for existing gender norms, community priorities, and local expertise. Third, participants highlighted fragmented coordination across sectors and organizations, advocating for stronger partnerships with women-led organizations (WLOs), improved collaboration, and more gender-balanced leadership. Finally, they emphasized the need to better institutionalize lessons learned and operationalize existing evidence to strengthen preparedness and support more effective, gender-informed responses during future outbreaks. CONCLUSIONS: To strengthen gender integration in global outbreak response, we recommend embedding GBV risk mitigation throughout response planning, improving cross-sector coordination and gender-balanced leadership, strengthening partnerships with local WLOs, and adopting flexible long-term funding mechanisms. Integrating gender-informed approaches into outbreak preparedness is essential to protecting women and girls, strengthening community resilience, and improving the effectiveness and sustainability of public health responses in LMICs.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the persistent gap in integrating gender-based violence (GBV) and gender-informed programming into outbreak preparedness and response in low- and middle-income countries (LMICs). It highlights the heightened GBV risks faced by women and girls during infectious disease outbreaks as a critical public-health problem necessitating targeted preparedness strategies.
Design and population
This qualitative study conducted in-depth interviews with ten public health practitioners experienced in supporting infectious disease outbreak preparedness and response in LMICs. Participants represented intergovernmental organizations and worked across multiple outbreak contexts, including Ebola, COVID-19, Mpox, Zika, and Cholera, in diverse geographic settings.
Core methods
The authors employed thematic content analysis using a mixed inductive and deductive coding approach to analyze interview data. Interviews were designed to examine barriers, gaps, successes, and opportunities for integrating gender-informed programming and GBV considerations into outbreak preparedness and response.
Main results
Four major themes emerged from the interviews: persistent exclusion of gender and GBV considerations due to competing priorities and inadequate funding; insufficient community engagement and localization; fragmented coordination across sectors; and the need to institutionalize lessons learned and operationalize existing evidence for future preparedness.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as evidence that gender integration in outbreak response remains underprioritized despite its critical role in protecting women and girls and strengthening community resilience. They emphasize the novelty of centering practitioner perspectives to identify actionable gaps and opportunities for systemic improvement.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that embedding GBV risk mitigation into response planning, improving cross-sector coordination, strengthening partnerships with women-led organizations, and adopting flexible long-term funding mechanisms could enhance the effectiveness and sustainability of public health responses in LMICs.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its small sample size of ten practitioners and reliance on qualitative methods, which may not capture the full range of barriers or successes across diverse contexts. The evidence strength is moderate due to the absence of quantitative validation or broader stakeholder perspectives.
